<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以压缩形式捕获湍流的多尺度统计特性是降阶建模的核心挑战。高度间歇性的被动标量在压缩表示中难以准确捕捉其非高斯、间歇性的统计特征，现有方法在此方面存在不足。

Method: 提出一种混合张量列（TT）方法，将标准TT分解与其他分解（如Galerkin、小波等）的优点相结合，专门针对高间歇性被动标量设计，以在压缩表示下同时兼顾多尺度结构函数和间歇性非高斯涨落的表征。

Result: 混合TT方法在结构函数方面与Galerkin、小波和标准TT分解性能相当，但在表征间歇性非高斯波动方面优于这些方法。

Conclusion: 该方法为直接在压缩张量形式下演化被动标量的线性动力学提供了可行路径，并可能启发现有流体输运问题的量子算法应用。

Abstract: Capturing the multiscale statistics of turbulence in compressed form remains a central challenge for reduced-order modeling. We introduce a hybrid Tensor Train (TT) approach for a highly intermittent passive scalar. The hybrid TT matches Galerkin, wavelet, and standard TT decompositions for the structure functions while improving the representation of intermittent, non-Gaussian fluctuations. These results open a route toward evolving the linear dynamics of passive scalars directly in compressed tensor form, with potential applications to quantum algorithms for fluid transport.

</details>


### [95] [Elasto-hydrodynamics of droplet-pool-interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00274)
*Md Sultan,Purbarun Dhar*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过实验、理论与模拟相结合，系统研究了弹性流体（如Boger流体）中液滴撞击液池所产生的空腔、冠状结构及Worthington射流的动力学行为，揭示了弹性储能、流体松弛与惯性-弹性-毛细力竞争对这些现象的控制作用，并发现了有别于牛顿流体的全新形态学机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在牛顿流体中，液滴撞击液池会产生空腔、冠状结构、毛细波和Worthington射流等丰富的流体动力学现象，但弹性流体或Boger流体中的对应行为此前几乎未被系统研究，属于理解与探索的空白领域。

Method: 采用实验、理论与数值模拟相结合的方法：(1) 在广泛的撞击Weber数和Deborah数范围内，对多种牛顿流体与弹性流体的液滴-液池组合进行系统性实验探究；(2) 在势流框架下基于能量守恒推导空腔半径演化理论；(3) 借助FENE-P模型推导细长Worthington射流半径的时间演化理论；(4) 通过计算机模拟进一步阐明弹性应力与局域速度场对空腔演化、形态及射流形成的控制作用。

Result: 发现了与牛顿流体相比全新的、截然不同的形态学区域，并证明这些发现纯粹由流体弹性驱动，而非黏度或界面张力所致。理论表明液滴撞击动能的30-40%可在空腔膨胀过程中以弹性势能形式被拉伸的聚合物链存储；在弹性-毛细力竞争区域，射流半径呈指数衰减。

Conclusion: 该研究显著推进了对惯性、毛细力与弹性在液滴-液池相互作用弹性流体动力学（elastohydrodynamics）中相互作用这一尚未探索范式的理解。

Abstract: In Newtonian fluids, impact of a droplet on a liquid pool births a cavity, crown, capillary waves, and Worthington jet. The corresponding hydrodynamic events for elastic or Boger fluids, however, remain an uncharted domain of comprehension and exploration. We thoroughly investigate, via experiments, theory, and simulations, how elastic energy storage, fluid relaxation, and competitive inertio elasto capillarity govern the spatio temporal evolution of the cavity, the crown, and the ensuing Worthington jet in polymeric elastic fluids. The events are systematically explored over a wide range of impact Weber and Deborah numbers, considering varied Newtonian and elastic fluid droplet pool combinations, and revealing new, and distinct morphological regimes compared to Newtonian counterparts. We illustrate that these new findings are purely driven by fluid elasticity, and not by viscosity or interfacial tension. We derive a theory for cavity radius evolution, using energy conservation within potential-flow framework. We show that 30-40 % of the droplets kinetic impact energy may be stored as elastic energy by the stretching polymer chains during cavity expansion. Appealing to the FENE P model, we derive a theory for the temporal evolution of the radius of the elongated Worthington jet. We show that in elasto capillary regime, competitive elastic and capillary stresses lead to exponential decay of the jet radius. The role of elastic stresses and the local velocity field in governing cavity evolution, morphology, and jet formation are further elucidated through computer simulations. Our findings significantly advance the uncharted paradigm of interplay between inertia, capillarity, and elasticity in droplet-pool interaction elastohydrodynamics.

</details>


### [96] [Buoyancy-driven melt rate of a vertical ice surface in seawater](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00731)
*Ho Yin Ng,Emily S. C. Ching*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出了湍流双扩散对流在无限垂直通道中的盐和热通量理论，并将其扩展为垂直冰面在海水中的浮力驱动融化速率的理论估算，理论结果与直接数值模拟和实验室实验数据吻合良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潮汐冰川的融化速率估算需要准确了解近垂直冰-洋界面的盐和热通量，但目前对此缺乏准确认识，亟需建立理论框架。

Method: 在无限垂直通道中建立湍流双扩散对流的盐热通量理论，进一步扩展该理论来推导垂直冰面在海水中的浮力驱动融化速率。

Result: 理论结果与直接数值模拟（DNS）数据以及实验室实验测量结果均表现出良好的一致性。

Conclusion: 所提出的理论能够为潮汐冰川垂直冰面的融化速率提供可靠的理论估算。

Abstract: Reliable estimates of the melt rate of tidewater glaciers require accurate knowledge of salt and heat fluxes at the near-vertical ice-ocean interface and such knowledge is currently lacking. In this paper, we present a theory for salt and heat fluxes in the idealized system of turbulent double-diffusive convection in an infinite vertical channel and show how this theory can be extended to give theoretical estimate of the buoyancy-driven melt rate of a vertical ice surface in seawater. Our theoretical results are shown to be in good agreement with direct numerical simulation data and measurements from laboratory experiments.

</details>


### [97] [Physics-informed neural networks for two-dimensional wall-reactive solute dispersion in canonical shear flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00856)
*Nanda Poddar,Subham Dhar*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于物理信息神经网络（PINN）的无网格框架，用于模拟剪切流（Couette、Poiseuille和Couette-Poiseuille）中由吸收壁边界限定的二维壁反应性溶质扩散，通过嵌入对流-扩散方程和Robin边界条件到统一损失函数中，重建时空浓度场，并与ADI有限差分基准对照验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典解析方法和基于网格的数值求解器虽然已广泛表征了剪切流中反应性溶质的输运机制，但在不对称反应性环境中精确解析溶质羽流的时空演化仍然计算代价高昂，因此需要更高效、可解释的计算工具。

Method: 构建物理信息神经网络（PINN）框架，将控制方程（对流-扩散方程）和Robin边界条件嵌入统一损失函数，在Pe=10条件下对三种典型剪切流（Couette、Poiseuille、Couette-Poiseuille）进行训练，覆盖不可渗透壁、对称吸收（β₁,β₂）=(1,1)以及十倍不对称壁反应性对比（0.2,2）和（2,0.2）等工况，并与交替方向隐式（ADI）有限差分基准进行对比验证。

Result: PINN预测结果与ADI有限差分基准在非反应性、对称反应性和不对称反应性三种工况下均高度吻合。利用训练后网络的可微特性，提取了表观轴向扩散系数、累积壁去除动力学和局部吸收通量等高分辨率输运诊断量。结果表明剪切廓线主导反应性吸收的流向组织，而不等壁反应性引入的横向不对称性会改变宏观扩散速率。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了PINN作为可解释的无网格工具在剪切流中边界耦合反应性输运分析中的有效性，为计算昂贵的反应性溶质输运模拟提供了新的替代方案。

Abstract: The dispersion of reactive solutes in shear flows is governed by the interplay between advective stretching, transverse diffusion, and boundary exchange kinetics. While classical analytical methods and grid-based numerical solvers have extensively characterised these transport mechanisms, accurately resolving the spatiotemporal evolution of solute plumes in asymmetric reactive environments remains computationally demanding. In this study, we introduce a physics-informed neural network (PINN) framework to simulate two-dimensional wall-reactive solute dispersion in canonical shear flows (Couette, Poiseuille, and Couette-Poiseuille) bounded by absorbing walls. By embedding the governing convection-diffusion equation and Robin boundary conditions into a unified loss function, the mesh-free PINN reconstructs the spatiotemporal concentration field. The network predictions are validated against an alternating-direction implicit (ADI) finite-difference benchmark, showing close agreement across non-reactive, symmetric, and asymmetric reactive regimes. The computations are carried out at $\mathrm{Pe}=10$ for impermeable walls, symmetric absorption $(β_1,β_2)=(1,1)$, and tenfold asymmetric wall-reactivity contrasts $(β_1,β_2)=(0.2,2)$ and (2,0.2). Leveraging the differentiable nature of the trained PINN, we extract wall-resolved transport diagnostics, including the apparent axial dispersion coefficient, cumulative wall-removal dynamics, and localised uptake fluxes. The results show that the imposed shear profile governs the streamwise organisation of reactive uptake, while unequal wall reactivities induce transverse asymmetry that modifies the macroscopic spreading rate. Overall, this framework establishes PINNs as an interpretable mesh-free tool for analysing boundary-coupled reactive transport in shear flows.

</details>


### [98] [4D Topology optimization of moving rigid bodies in fluid flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01071)
*Yuta Tanabe,Kentaro Yaji,Kuniharu Ushijima*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种4D拓扑优化框架，用于同时优化刚性体的形状与运动，以诱导流体流动。形状通过伪密度法在设计网格上表示，运动由离散时间步位置参数化并经过时间平滑处理，流体动力学采用格子动力学方案（LBM扩展）评估，通过伴随变量法推导设计灵敏度，并在优化过程中同步更新形状与运动。通过2D和3D数值算例验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的拓扑优化方法通常只优化系统形状或只在给定运动下优化形状，无法同时设计刚体的形态与运动轨迹。本文旨在提出一个统一的框架，允许形状与运动在同一优化过程中协同设计，从而获得更优的流体诱导系统性能。

Method: 采用4D拓扑优化框架：(1) 形状表示在设计网格（独立于分析网格），通过伪密度方法描述；(2) 运动直接以离散时间步位置参数化，并采用时间滤波技术平滑；(3) 流体动力学通过格子动力学方案（LBM的扩展版本）求解；(4) 对形状和运动的设计灵敏度通过伴随变量法推导；(5) 优化过程中形状与运动同步更新。

Result: 给出了二维和三维数值算例，并从物理角度进行了讨论。通过与仅优化形状或仅优化运动的对比案例以及多项参数研究，证明了所提出方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 4D拓扑优化框架能够同时优化刚性体的形状与运动，相比单独优化形状或运动的方法能获得更优的设计结果，为流体诱导系统设计提供了新的工具。

Abstract: This study applies $\textit{4D topology optimization}$, a framework for simultaneously optimizing the morphology and motion of a system, to a rigid body that induces fluid flow. The rigid body shape is represented on a design grid that is independent of the analysis grid, and at each time step, it undergoes rigid-body motion before being mapped onto the analysis grid. The shape is represented using a pseudo-density method, while the motion is directly parametrized by the positions at discrete time steps and smoothed using a temporal filtering technique. The fluid dynamics are evaluated through the lattice kinetic scheme, an extended version of the lattice Boltzmann method. Design sensitivities with respect to both shape and motion are derived via the adjoint variable method, and the shape and motion are intentionally updated simultaneously during the optimization process. Finally, two- and three-dimensional numerical examples are presented and discussed from a physical perspective. Furthermore, the effectiveness of the proposed method is demonstrated by comparison with cases in which only the shape or motion is optimized, as well as through several parameter studies.

</details>


### [99] [Stable and Efficient One-Way Modelling of Convective Disturbances in Laminar Boundary Layers: OWNS-Summation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01155)
*Elliot J. Badcock,Shahid Mughal*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出OWNS-S（OWNS求和）公式，将单向空间推进Navier-Stokes方法中的谱投影器有理逼近从递归乘积形式重构为加性部分分式求和形式，消除了递归OWNS-R中舍入误差的乘性放大问题，使逼近阶N成为纯收敛参数且可并行计算，并在不可压缩、高超声速和跨声速边界层中验证了其准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的递归型OWNS（OWNS-R）将谱投影器的有理逼近表示为N个预解因子的乘积，但乘积形式会乘性放大舍入误差，导致逼近阶N存在流场依赖的上限，且该上限无法事先确定，这严重限制了方法的鲁棒性和可预测性。

Method: 将相同的谱投影器有理逼近重构为加性部分分式求和形式（OWNS-S）。每个预解求解作用于相同的输入状态并独立贡献于加权和，从而避免乘性误差放大。此外引入配对贪心参数选择过程，候选参数来自上游和下游谱区域的解析估计，避免数值推进过程中的特征分解。

Result: 在精确算术中等价于OWNS-R（给定相同权重和极点），浮点算术中两者不等价。OWNS-S使用与OWNS-R相同的辅助极点时在所有测试配置中保持准确，证明递归求值而非极点选择是主要不稳定源。该方法在不可压缩、高超声速和跨声速边界层中得到验证，其中跨声速情况下扰动谱在推进过程中从亚声速拓扑重组为超声速拓扑，单向计算可在抛物化稳定性方程无法达到的流向分辨率下连续通过该转变。

Conclusion: OWNS-S通过加性求和消除递归乘法算子固有的数值不稳定性，使逼近阶N成为纯收敛参数，支持并行化，提升了单向Navier-Stokes方法的数值鲁棒性、可预测性和计算效率，尤其适用于包含谱拓扑转变的复杂跨声速流动。

Abstract: One-way spatial marching methods separate upstream- from downstream-propagating disturbances using a rational approximation of a spectral projector. Among existing one-way Navier--Stokes (OWNS) formulations, the recursive variant, OWNS-R, is the most economical, evaluating the approximation as a product of $N$ resolvent factors. This product amplifies rounding errors multiplicatively, imposing a flow-dependent upper limit on the approximation order that cannot be known in advance.
  We reformulate the same approximation as an additive partial-fraction sum (OWNS-Summation, OWNS-S). In exact arithmetic, the recursive and summation evaluations are equivalent when supplied with identical weights and poles; in floating-point arithmetic, they are not. Each of the $N$ resolvent solves acts on the same input state and contributes independently to a weighted sum, preventing multiplicative error amplification. The approximation order $N$ therefore becomes a pure convergence parameter, and the solves can run in parallel.
  Using the same auxiliary poles as OWNS-R, OWNS-S remains accurate in every configuration tested, showing that the recursive evaluation, rather than the poles, is the dominant source of instability. A paired greedy parameter-selection procedure is also introduced, with candidates drawn from analytic estimates of the upstream and downstream spectral regions, avoiding eigen-decomposition during the numerical march.
  OWNS-S is validated on incompressible, hypersonic and transonic boundary layers. In the transonic case, the disturbance spectrum reorganises from a subsonic to a supersonic topology during the march. The one-way computation proceeds continuously through this transition at a streamwise resolution unattainable by the parabolised stability equations.

</details>


### [100] [Cross-stream pressure support and the limits of scalar relaxation in rarefied Poiseuille flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01224)
*Omid Ejtehadi,Ehsan Roohi*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文研究了稀薄压力驱动Poiseuille流动中壁面法向压力变化这一高阶本构模型的严格检验问题。通过DSMC模拟与二维动量预算分析，发现非平衡法向应力过度支撑测得的压力亏缺，而剪切应力的流向输运提供反向修正。研究揭示应力-动量补偿机制使得简化本构律即使内部闭合机制不正确，仍能在弱非平衡观测量上获得一致结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望解决切线形式非线性耦合本构关系(NCCR)在定量上成功复现稀薄Poiseuille流压力凸型拓扑的物理原因之谜——即本构应力关系与约化流向强迫是否各自独立准确，还是其效应相互补偿。这一问题的澄清对高阶本构模型的可靠性与验证至关重要。

Method: 采用直接模拟蒙特卡洛(DSMC)大规模计算，配合多种诊断手段：基于横向信号的二维动量预算分析、压力误差范数、匹配出口Knudsen数的对比研究，以及分量的预消元NCCR平衡检验。

Result: 发现非平衡法向应力过度支撑压力亏缺，而剪切应力流向输运提供相反修正；出口Knudsen数与出口Mach数均不能单独组织压力振幅；DSMC推断的应力关系偏离固定约化系数，且最佳公共标量闭合剪切主分量远比承载压力场的法向分量准确。仅修正系数反而恶化重建，而恢复被省略的流向动量项能在强加速情况下减小振幅偏差。

Conclusion: 简化的本构律可通过应力-动量补偿保持准确性，这揭示了一个重要方法论警示：弱非平衡观测量的一致并不一定意味着内部闭合机制正确，模型的成功可能源于误差抵消而非物理机制的真实再现。

Abstract: The weak wall-normal pressure variation in pressure-driven rarefied Poiseuille flow is a stringent test of higher-order constitutive models: it is almost invisible in the total-pressure norm, yet it is generated by anisotropic molecular stress. A tangent-form nonlinear coupled constitutive relation (NCCR) reproduces its convex topology, but the physical reason for its quantitative success remains unresolved. We ask whether the constitutive stress relation and reduced streamwise forcing are independently accurate or whether their effects compensate. A direct simulation Monte Carlo (DSMC) campaign is analysed using two-dimensional momentum budgets, a pressure-error norm based on the transverse signal, a matched-outlet-Knudsen comparison and componentwise tests of the pre-elimination NCCR balance. The non-equilibrium wall-normal stress over-supports the measured pressure defect, while streamwise transport of shear stress supplies an opposing correction. The state map, momentum budgets and forcing diagnostics show that neither outlet Knudsen nor outlet Mach number alone organises the pressure amplitude; along the fixed-ratio sequence, rarefaction is accompanied by larger changes in the constitutive diagnostics than in pressure amplitude. The DSMC-inferred stress relation departs from the fixed reduced coefficient, and even the best common scalar closes the dominant shear component far more accurately than the normal components that carry the pressure field. Correcting the coefficient alone can therefore worsen the reconstruction, whereas restoring omitted streamwise-momentum terms reduces the amplitude bias in strongly accelerated cases. The reduced law can remain accurate through stress--momentum compensation, showing that agreement of a weak non-equilibrium observable need not imply correct internal closure mechanics.

</details>


### [101] [Supersonic jet impingement on concave surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01542)
*Hemanth Chandravamsi,Dhanush Vittal Shenoy,Steven H. Frankel*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过可压缩大涡模拟（LES）、涡面模型和Powell反馈环分析，研究圆形超声速射流撞击凹面壁的声气动共振。相比平壁参考，凹面壁使主音调幅值最多增加23 dB，增幅源于马赫盘声源幅值增强及上游反馈波更高效地返回喷嘴。螺旋模式频率与涡面模型预测的导引射流模式吻合，而轴对称频率遵循Powell经典环路长度准则，表明二者采用不同的频率选择机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声速射流冲击凹面结构在航空航天推进系统（如垂直起降飞行器、火箭发射）中普遍存在，会产生强烈的气动噪声和结构载荷。现有研究多聚焦射流冲击平壁，对凹面几何如何影响声共振、反馈回路及频率选择机制的理解不足，需系统阐明凹面曲率对声气动共振幅值和频率的作用机理。

Method: 采用可压缩大涡模拟（LES）对理想膨胀马赫数1.56、雷诺数6×10⁴的窒息射流进行数值模拟；使用涡面模型（vortex-sheet model）预测导引喷流模式（guided jet modes）的频率与径向特征函数；结合Powell反馈环分析及Powell-Tam声源-传递预算方法，量化凹面壁对声源幅值和反馈波返回效率的贡献。研究包含两块平壁（L/D=2.08和2.58）及四个不同凹陷参数σ（0.4, 0.8, 1.6, 4.0）的高斯凹面构型。

Result: 当凹陷收窄时，主音调幅值相比L/D=2.6的平壁参考最多增加23 dB，同时壁面压力脉动和力矩增大。Powell-Tam预算表明放大归因于马赫盘声源幅值增强及上游反馈波更高效地返回喷嘴；凹面壁的声聚焦效应解释更强的上游传播波。螺旋模式的测量频率和径向特征函数与涡面模型预测的导引喷流模式高度一致，且该模式在凹面和平壁上均被选择，表明其由等效理想膨胀射流的剪切层剖面控制而非壁面几何。轴对称频率则无法匹配任何导引模式分支，而遵循Powell经典环路长度准则。

Conclusion: 研究工作揭示了超声速射流冲击凹面壁时螺旋和轴对称啸声（screech）采用截然不同的频率选择机制：螺旋模式由射流剪切层固有导引模式决定，轴对称模式由全局声反馈环路长度决定。壁面曲率可作为有效控制啸声幅值和表面载荷的手段，为凹面壁冲击射流噪声控制和结构设计提供理论指导。

Abstract: The aeroacoustic resonance of round supersonic jets impinging on concave surfaces is investigated using compressible large-eddy simulations, vortex-sheet modelling, and Powell's feedback-loop analysis. The choked jets operate at an ideally expanded Mach number of $1.56$ and a Reynolds number of $6\times10^4$. Six geometries are considered: two flat plates at $L/D=2.08$ and $2.58$, where $L$ is the nozzle-to-wall distance and $D$ the nozzle exit diameter, and four Gaussian concave surfaces of fixed depth and indentation spread $σ\in\{0.4,0.8,1.6,4.0\}$. As the indentation narrows, the primary-tone amplitude increases by up to $23\,\mathrm{dB}$ relative to the flat-wall reference at $L/D=2.6$, together with larger wall-pressure fluctuations and moments. A Powell-Tam source-transfer budget attributes this amplification to increased Mach-disk source amplitude and more efficient return of the upstream feedback wave to the nozzle. The stronger upstream-propagating waves are consistent with acoustic focusing by the concave wall. For the helical cases, the measured frequencies and radial eigenfunctions agree closely with the guided jet mode predicted by the vortex-sheet model, supporting its role in closing the upstream feedback path. The same selection is recovered for concave and flat walls alike, so this tone is governed by the shear-layer profile of the equivalent ideally expanded jet rather than by the wall geometry. The axisymmetric frequencies, by contrast, coincide with no guided-mode branch and appear instead to follow Powell's classical loop-length criterion. The results identify distinct frequency-selection mechanisms for helical and axisymmetric screech and demonstrate that wall curvature provides effective control of screech amplitude and surface loading.

</details>


### [102] [Leidenfrost droplets: The roles of ambient humidity and internal droplet circulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01828)
*Maxim de Wildt,Andrea Prosperetti,Christian Diddens,Detlef Lohse*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过直接数值模拟，在统一计算模型下分析了跨越四个数量级半径的Leidenfrost水滴特性，发现环境湿度与液滴内部流动对几何形貌和干燥动力学有显著影响，并揭示了轴对称模型导致与大液滴实验结果的重大偏差，进而通过方位稳定性分析和3D模拟验证了非轴对称效应的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关于Leidenfrost效应的研究分别聚焦于大液滴和小液滴极限，缺乏统一模型下跨尺寸尺度的系统分析；同时环境湿度在Leidenfrost体系中的作用长期被忽视，且常用的纯蒸汽相和等温液滴假设可能存在不足。

Method: 采用直接数值模拟（DNS）建立单一计算模型，覆盖液滴半径四个数量级范围；使用轴对称模型模拟大液滴并与实验对比发现偏差，进而通过方位稳定性分析（azimuthal stability analysis）证实轴对称约束的非物理性，最终用简化模型的3D模拟进一步支持该结论。

Result: 环境湿度与液滴内部流动对Leidenfrost液滴的几何形貌和干燥动力学有显著影响；纯蒸汽相与等温液滴假设不充分；轴对称模型在大液滴模拟中与实验存在较大偏差，该偏差源于轴对称约束；3D模拟支持了方位不稳定性是导致偏差的原因。

Conclusion: Leidenfrost液滴的准确建模需要考虑环境湿度、非等温效应以及非轴对称三维现象；针对剩余实验偏差，提出了进一步可探索的假设。

Abstract: A volatile droplet gently deposited on a superheated substrate can sit on a thin film of its own vapour, which prevents contact between the drop and surface. This phenomenon is called the Leidenfrost effect. In this paper, through direct numerical simulations, we analyse characteristics of Leidenfrost water droplets with a single computational model over four decades of droplet radius, stitching together previous works in the limit of large and small droplets. Using the model, we show that the ambient humidity, an underappreciated factor in the Leidenfrost system, in combination with the flow in the drop has a significant impact on the geometry and drying kinetics. Our results imply the inadequacies of commonly made assumptions of a pure vapour phase and an isothermal droplet. When modelling large Leidenfrost droplets with an axisymmetric model, large discrepancies between experiments and the computational results occur. Through azimuthal stability analysis, we show that this is due to the unrealistic constraint of axisymmetry. This finding is supported by 3D simulations of a simplified model. Finally, some hypotheses are explored to account for the remaining discrepancies with experimental data.

</details>


### [103] [Measurements of non-linear energy transfer in canonical and drag-reduced turbulent boundary layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02003)
*Max W. Knoop,Bas W. van Oudheusden,Rahul Deshpande*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文利用三维粒子追踪测速(3D-PTV)技术测量并计算了湍流边界层中雷诺应力张量的谱输运，首次对零压力梯度(ZPG)湍流边界层中流向雷诺应力的非线性输运项进行分量级分解，揭示了与展向和壁法向平流相关的不同能量传递机制，并将该框架应用于减阻约38%的湍流边界层流场，发现壁面强迫导致非线性能量传递的强烈衰减及远离壁面的偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解湍流边界层中雷诺应力各分量的能量传递机制对于揭示湍流物理和优化减阻控制策略至关重要。以往研究缺乏对非线性输运项的分量级分解，难以区分不同方向平流对能量传递的贡献；同时需要验证实验框架在高雷诺数下的可靠性，以支撑流动物理结论。

Method: 采用三维粒子追踪测速(3D-PTV)实验测量，在摩擦雷诺数Reτ=1020的零压力梯度湍流边界层中验证实验框架的充分性，对非线性输运项进行分量级分解；随后将该实验框架应用于稳态展向交替壁面速度驱动的减阻湍流边界层流动中，对比分析减阻前后非线性能量传递的变化。

Result: 在ZPG湍流边界层中，流向雷诺应力的非线性输运项首次被分解为展向与壁法向平流相关的独立能量传递机制。在减阻流动中，非线性能量传递被强烈衰减并远离壁面偏移，但能量传递机制的定性特征与标准ZPG湍流边界层相似，表明现有机制只是重新调整以适应新的低湍动能状态。

Conclusion: 实验框架能够充分解析主要的非线性能量传递机制以支撑流动物理结论；壁面强迫减阻并未改变能量传递机制的定性本质，而是通过衰减和移位实现减阻效果，这为基于能量传递调控的减阻策略提供了物理基础。

Abstract: Three-dimensional particle-tracking velocimetry (3D-PTV) measurements were used to compute the spectral transport of the Reynolds-stress tensor. The experimental framework is validated for a zero-pressure-gradient (ZPG) turbulent boundary layer (TBL) at a friction Reynolds number $Re_τ= 1020$, demonstrating that the dominant non-linear energy transfer mechanisms are adequately resolved to draw flow physics-based conclusions. For the streamwise Reynolds stress in the ZPG TBL, a component-wise decomposition of the non-linear transport term is considered for the first time, which reveals distinct energy transfer mechanisms associated with the spanwise and wall-normal advection. The same experimental framework was applied to a drag-reduced ($\approx 38\%$) TBL flow, achieved by imposing a steady streamwise-alternating spanwise wall velocity. This wall forcing causes a strong attenuation of non-linear energy transfer and its shift away from the wall. The energy transfer mechanisms remain qualitatively similar to those of the canonical ZPG TBL, suggesting that the existing mechanisms simply readjust to their new low-turbulent-energy state.

</details>


### [104] [Coherent structure modulation and recovery in drag-reduced turbulent boundary layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02017)
*Traian Bistriceanu,Vyacheslav A. Kovalyov,Pradyumn Mishra,Roann A. A. Kerbergen,Max W. Knoop,Bas W. van Oudheusden*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文基于Knoop等人的实验，利用粒子跟踪测速数据，分析了稳态方波展向壁面速度强迫在不同流向波长下对湍流减阻的影响。研究发现该强迫在各波长下均抑制近壁喷射，而外层扫掠抑制随波长增强；在次优波长后出现流向周期性的湍流衰减与恢复现象，该恢复由极小尺度结构的增强及其引发的破裂事件驱动，且这些事件具有普遍性特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解展向壁面速度方波强迫在不同流向波长下实现减阻的湍流调制机制，特别是揭示后最优条件下湍流恢复现象的物理根源，为优化减阻控制策略提供机理层面的认识。

Method: 采用粒子跟踪测速（PTV）技术获取流动数据，对非激励参考工况和振幅A+=12的激励工况进行对比分析，覆盖三个流向强迫波长（Λx+=471, 942, 1884）。使用条件平均场分析大尺度湍动能爆发事件，并结合小波分析研究不同尺度结构的空间演化特征。

Result: 强迫在所有波长下均抑制近壁喷射，外层扫掠抑制随波长增大而增强。后最优波长下出现流向周期性的湍流衰减与恢复，恢复由远小于壁湍流典型能量尺度的极小尺度结构增强所驱动。小尺度破裂事件频率由近最优到后最优增加四倍，且表现出统一的特征与强度。小波分析显示后最优工况下极小尺度在壁面速度恒定的区域内沿流向逐步增强，驱动了周期性小尺度再能源与抑制的循环。

Conclusion: 展向壁面速度方波强迫对湍流的调制具有尺度选择性：小尺度结构周期性涌现，而大尺度运动随强迫波长增大被更有效地抑制。极小尺度破裂事件的统一特征暗示了恢复现象可能的普遍性，为流动控制中的尺度耦合机制提供了新见解。

Abstract: Drag reduction is achieved by a steady square-wave forcing of the spanwise wall velocity, based on the experiments in Knoop et al. (Phys. Rev. Fluids, 10, 2025). Particle tracking velocimetry data are analyzed for a non-actuated reference case and actuation at forcing amplitude $A^+ = 12$ ($+$ denotes viscous scaling) for three streamwise forcing wavelengths, which correspond to sub-optimal ($Λ_x^+ = 471$), near-optimal ($Λ_x^+ = 942$), and post-optimal ($Λ_x^+ = 1884$) drag reduction conditions. Conditionally averaged fields on large-scale bursts of turbulent kinetic energy show that forcing suppresses near-wall ejections across all cases, while outer-layer sweep suppression strengthens with $Λ_x^+$. Post-optimal forcing exhibits streamwise-periodic attenuation and recovery of turbulence. The recovery phenomenon is caused by an enhancement of very small scales, significantly smaller than those typically energetic in wall turbulence, and is linked to the emergence of small-scale bursting events. While these small-scale bursts are statistically insignificant in the non-actuated and sub-optimal cases, their frequency increases by a factor of four between the near-optimal and post-optimal cases. The small-scale bursts exhibit uniform signatures and intensities, hinting at the possible universality of the recovery phenomenon. A wavelet analysis shows that, in the post-optimal case, very small scales increase progressively in the streamwise direction in regions where the wall velocity remains constant, driving a cyclic pattern of small-scale re-energization and suppression consistent with earlier statistical analysis. This turbulence modulation mechanism is scale-selective: while small-scale structures emerge periodically, large-scale motions are more effectively suppressed as the forcing wavelength increases.

</details>


### [105] [Aerodynamic Drag and Heat Transfer Corrections for Dehydrated Pollen Particles: CFD-Based Modeling of Airborne Allergen Transport in Smart Urban Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02360)
*Omar Hamad,Samer Ali,Mahmoud Khaled,Talib Dbouk*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文首次通过高级CFD模拟，定量评估了真实脱水（干燥）花粉颗粒的气动阻力和对流换热特性。在雷诺数0.1至15范围内，干燥花粉的阻力系数比水合球形花粉高8%至15%，而努塞尔数低5%至15%，表明传统球形假设不适用于干花粉的拉格朗日输运和蒸发模拟，城市过敏原输运建模需考虑真实脱水形态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市空气质量评估、过敏风险预测和智慧城市规划高度依赖花粉气溶胶输运模拟。然而，传统弥散模型通常假设花粉为光滑球形颗粒，忽略了花粉脱水引起的形态变化及其对空气动力学和热力学行为的影响，导致模拟结果与实际情况存在偏差。

Method: 采用先进的计算流体力学（CFD）模拟方法，对真实脱水后的干燥花粉颗粒进行颗粒尺度分析，计算其气动阻力系数和对流换热（努塞尔数）。研究覆盖雷诺数范围0.1≤Re_p≤15，对应实际大气风速0.27至30 km/h。

Result: 干燥花粉颗粒的阻力系数比水合球形花粉的预测值高出8%至15%；而其努塞尔数比水合球形花粉低5%至15%。两者均呈现显著偏离，证实传统球形关联式无法准确描述干燥花粉的输运与蒸发行为。

Conclusion: 传统球形颗粒假设不足以模拟干燥花粉的拉格朗日输运和蒸发过程。城市环境中空气传播过敏原的建模必须考虑花粉真实脱水后的非球形形态，以提高预测精度。

Abstract: Airborne pollen transport is a key concern for urban air-quality assessment, allergy-risk forecasting, and smart-city planning. However, conventional dispersion models generally assume smooth spherical particles, neglecting how pollen dehydration alters particle morphology and impacts aerodynamic and thermal behavior. To address this gap, this study presents, for the first time, advanced CFD simulations evaluating the aerodynamic drag forces and convective heat transfer of realistically dehydrated (dry) pollen particles. Investigations are conducted at Reynolds numbers ($0.1 \leq Re_p \leq 15$) at the particle's scale corresponding to realistic atmospheric wind speeds ranging from 0.27 to 30 km/h. The findings reveal that dry pollen particles exhibit drag coefficients 8% to 15% higher than those predicted for hydrated pollen spherical particles. Conversely, their Nusselt numbers are 5% to 15% lower than those for hydrated pollen particles. These considerable deviations confirm that conventional spherical correlations are inadequate for simulating dry pollen Lagrangian transport and evaporation. These findings highlight the need to account for realistic dehydrated shapes when modeling airborne allergen transport in urban environments.

</details>
