### [106] [Mechanism of State Transitions for the Vector Kuznetsov-Ma Breathers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00570)
*Muchen Dong,Lei Wang*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 本文研究了耦合Fokas-Lenells框架下矢量Kuznetsov-Ma呼吸子在不等背景上的两种状态转变机制，揭示了简并区非自共轭退化曲线两侧产生不连续呼吸子-孤子跃迁，以及非简并区背景平面波在特殊波数处频率匹配导致呼吸子转化为单孤子或双孤子态的转变行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解矢量Kuznetsov-Ma呼吸子在不同背景下从呼吸子态向孤子态的转变机制，特别是振幅不平衡如何打破谱反射对称性并产生更丰富的呼吸子形态，以及不同退化区域中状态转变的物理机理。

Method: 通过理论分析研究耦合Fokas-Lenells框架中的状态转变机制，包括区分简并与非简并扇区，分析非自共轭退化曲线两侧的极限行为，确定状态转变阈值，并利用数值激励方法验证理论结果。

Result: 发现简并扇区中KMB从非自共轭退化曲线两侧趋近时产生不同的极限孤子，呈现不连续的KMB到孤子的转变；非简并扇区中背景平面波在特殊波数处达到频率匹配，使KMB转化为单孤子或双孤子态；精确确定了转变阈值并描述了邻近解的参数依赖性；还研究了自共轭退化曲线及实谱上转变波数处的特殊极限机制，数值结果进一步支持了这些发现。

Conclusion: 该研究完整刻画了耦合Fokas-Lenells系统中矢量KMB的两类状态转变机制，揭示了简并与非简并扇区中截然不同的转变行为，为理解非线性波系统中呼吸子与孤子之间的动态转换提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We study two types of mechanisms of state transitions for vector Kuznetsov-Ma breathers (KMBs) in the coupled Fokas-Lenells framework on unequal backgrounds. The amplitude imbalance breaks spectral reflection symmetry and generates richer breather morphologies. In the degenerate sector, KMBs approaching a non-self-conjugate degeneration curve from opposite sides yield different limiting solitons, revealing a discontinuous KMB-to-soliton transition. In the nondegenerate sector, the background plane waves become frequency matched at a special wavenumber, converting the KMB into a single- or two-soliton state. We determine the exact transition threshold and characterize how nearby solutions vary with the parameter. We further investigate special limiting mechanisms arising on the self-conjugate degeneration curve and on the real spectrum at the state-transition wavenumber. Numerical excitation provides further evidence for these results.

</details>


### [107] [Duck hunting with quantum mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00579)
*Artem Alexandrov*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 本文建立了奇异摄动理论中慢快系统的经典理论与量子力学半经典方法之间的联系，证明经典奇异对象——canard解（鸭解）——是相应量子系统中瞬子（instantons）的经典阴影。以过阻尼约瑟夫森结为例，证明了canard解存在于由瞬子作用量决定的参数域中，该窗口对应夏皮罗台阶之间指数级狭窄的间隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将奇异摄动理论的两个看似独立的方面——经典慢快系统理论与量子半经典方法——统一起来，并揭示经典奇异对象（canard解）与量子现象（瞬子）之间的深层数学物理联系。

Method: 从理论上将canard解的参数域边界与瞬子作用量联系起来，并以过阻尼约瑟夫森结为具体物理实例进行解析推导和数值验证。

Result: 证明了canard解存在于由瞬子作用量确定的参数域内；对于约瑟夫森结，该canard窗口恰好是相邻夏皮罗台阶之间指数级狭窄的间隙。

Conclusion: 经典canard解可被视为量子瞬子的经典阴影，这一发现为两类理论建立了新的桥梁，并通过约瑟夫森结实例给出了清晰的物理解释。

Abstract: We bridge two sides of singular perturbation theory: the classical theory of slow-fast systems and the semi-classical approach to quantum mechanical systems. For a specific but physically important class of dynamical systems, we show that purely classical and exotic objects, so-called canard solutions, are shadows of instantons in the corresponding quantum system. We demonstrate that canard solutions exist in a domain of parameter space whose boundaries are determined by an instanton action. We illustrate our statements analytically for the relevant example, the overdamped Josephson junction, and confirm them numerically. For the Josephson junction, the canard window is the exponentially narrow gap between consecutive Shapiro steps.

</details>


### [108] [Hyperbolic-Tangent Shocks in a Lossy Nonlinear Transmission Line](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00723)
*Eugene Kogan*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 本文研究有损非线性传输线中的行波阵面（traveling fronts）。通过预设双曲正切轮廓（无量纲宽度为m），求解逆问题确定电压-电荷关系，使得该轮廓成为精确解，其精确关系用高斯超几何函数表示。同时，通过假设三次电压-电荷关系并将二次阻尼函数替换为其最优线性最小二乘逼近，构造了近似解。两种构造中，物理可容性选择激波阵面。在宽阵面极限下，近似归一化力在m^{-2}阶上与精确结果一致；数值比较表明即使远离大m的渐近区域，一致性仍然良好。最后，对原始偏微分方程的特征分析区分了压缩激波与非压缩扭结，无需借助力学类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有损非线性传输线中行波解的结构和性质，特别是确定何种电压-电荷关系能支持特定的行波轮廓，以及如何区分不同类型的波阵面（激波与扭结）。

Method: （1）逆问题方法：预设双曲正切轮廓，反解电压-电荷关系，得到高斯超几何函数形式的精确解；（2）近似方法：假设三次电压-电荷关系，用最优线性最小二乘逼近替换二次阻尼函数，确定三次系数并得到修正方程；（3）特征分析方法：对原始偏微分方程进行特征分析以区分压缩激波和非压缩扭结。

Result: 精确关系以高斯超几何函数表达；近似解在宽阵面极限下与精确结果在m^{-2}阶上一致，且在渐近区域外数值上仍保持良好一致性；物理可容性条件选出激波阵面；特征分析无需力学类比即可区分压缩激波与非压缩扭结。

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了一套从预设行波轮廓出发反推物理参数的完整框架，既给出了精确解又提供了高精度的近似构造，并通过特征分析为波阵面类型的判别提供了不依赖力学类比的直接方法。

Abstract: We investigate traveling fronts in a lossy nonlinear transmission line. By prescribing a hyperbolic-tangent profile with dimensionless width $m$, we solve the inverse problem of determining the voltage--charge relation for which this profile is an exact solution. The exact relation is expressed in terms of the Gauss hypergeometric function. We also construct an approximate solution by assuming a cubic voltage--charge relation and replacing the resulting quadratic damping function with its optimal linear least-squares approximation. This construction determines the cubic coefficients and yields a modified governing equation that admits the prescribed profile as an exact solution. In both constructions, physical admissibility selects a shock front. In the broad-front limit, the approximate normalized force agrees with the exact result through order $m^{-2}$; numerical comparisons show that the agreement remains good even outside the asymptotic region of large $m$. Finally, a characteristic analysis of the original partial differential equations distinguishes compressive shocks from noncompressive kinks without recourse to the mechanical analogy.

</details>


<div id='cs.SD'></div>

# cs.SD [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [Hidden-Domain Routing for All-Type Audio Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00493)
*Yifan Gao,Yao Tian,Hongbin Suo,Haonan Lu*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出一种封闭条件下的路由系统用于全类型音频伪造检测，先通过AudioType-BEATs-6s路由器恢复隐藏音频域，再利用各域专家模型进行分支内分数解读，在AT-ADD Track2最终评测中以96.10%的Macro-F1排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全类型音频深度伪造检测（涵盖语音、环境声、歌声和音乐）面临一个关键挑战：推理时音频类型不可用，但检测器分数的行为随音频类型变化。因此需要先恢复隐藏的音频域条件，才能正确解释检测分数。

Method: 采用封闭条件路由系统：AudioType-BEATs-6s路由器（基于6秒窗口的BEATs编码器）首先估计音频类型；语音输入由Speech-XLSR专家处理，而环境声、歌声和音乐则由基于EAT的通用音频专家处理，每个分支采用局部分数解读策略。

Result: 在官方AT-ADD Track2最终评测中，系统达到96.10%的Track2 Macro-F1，位列最终排行榜第一；各类型Macro-F1分别为语音88.07%、环境声98.18%、歌声99.07%、音乐99.08%。开发集上的表征分析和路由器家族对比实验验证了音频域分离性和各检测器在不同音频类型上的互补优势。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，在全类型音频深度伪造检测中，先恢复隐藏音频域再进行检测器分数解读是有效且必要的策略，该路由系统在跨音频类型的真实/伪造二元分类任务中展现了优越性能。

Abstract: All-type audio deepfake detection requires authenticity decisions across speech, environmental sound, singing voice, and music, while the audio type is unavailable at inference time. In AT-ADD Track2, this setting creates a hidden audio-domain condition: the binary real/fake label is shared across domains, but representation structure and detector-score behavior vary with audio type. We present a closed-condition routed system that first recovers the hidden audio domain and then interprets detector scores within the selected branch. The AudioType-BEATs-6s Router estimates audio type from a 6-second window; speech inputs are handled by the Speech-XLSR Expert, while sound, singing, and music rely on EAT-based general-audio experts with branch-local score interpretation. Development-set representation analysis, router-family comparisons, and component results show audio-domain separation and complementary detector strengths across audio types. On the official AT-ADD Track2 final evaluation, the system achieves 96.10% Track2 Macro-F1 and ranks first on the final leaderboard, with type-wise Macro-F1 scores of 88.07%, 98.18%, 99.07%, and 99.08% for speech, sound, singing, and music, respectively. These results support recovering the hidden audio domain before interpreting detector scores in all-type audio deepfake detection.

</details>


### [110] [Beyond Prompt Adherence: Auditing Attribute-Level Voice Control in Speech Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00545)
*Xianhao Zhou,Jianghao Wu*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文对三个语音生成系统进行配对审计，发现自然语言描述驱动的声音生成在符合目标方向的同时常常伴随目标集之外的信号级变化，并提出训练无关的候选选择方法VoDER-Cal，在不增加额外训练的情况下提升保持度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音生成评估主要关注输出是否匹配提示词，但提示匹配无法反映提示目标之外的特性是否保持稳定。作者认为需要一种"保持敏感"的评估方式，以揭示自然语言控制语音生成时的非预期变化。

Method: 对CosyVoice3、VoxCPM2和Fish-Speech-S2三个系统进行受控配对审计，使用5,940个输出（涵盖6位参考说话人、10段文本、3个随机种子、11种条件），通过声学、韵律、内容和说话人测量指标进行评估，并引入VoDER-Cal——一种无需训练的候选选择器，在保持目标响应强度的同时偏好更小的目标外偏差。

Result: 目标方向上的响应常伴随目标集之外的信号级变化，且不同系统间差异显著。将候选池从1个扩展到3个后，联合成功率从4.8%提升至约14%；VoDER-Cal将留出目标外偏差从0.344降至0.276，并改善了听者评级的保持度。

Conclusion: 保持敏感评估是提示遵循评估的重要补充，候选重排提供了实用的推理时改进手段。

Abstract: Natural-language descriptions have become a flexible interface for controlling generated speech. Existing evaluations largely assess whether an output matches a prompt, but prompt matching alone does not reveal whether characteristics outside the intended change remain stable. We examine this distinction through a controlled paired audit of three speech-generation systems: CosyVoice3, VoxCPM2, and Fish-Speech-S2. The evaluation contains 5,940 outputs spanning six reference speakers, ten texts, three random seeds, and eleven conditions. Using acoustic, prosodic, content, and speaker measurements, we find that responses in the expected target direction are frequently accompanied by changes outside descriptor-specific signal-level target sets. This pattern remains among outputs whose target response exceeds baseline seed variation, and the accompanying changes differ substantially across systems. We further introduce VoDER-Cal, a training-free candidate selector that retains sufficiently strong target responses while favoring smaller off-target deviations. A three-candidate pool raises the joint success rate from 4.8% under single-sample direct generation to approximately 14% for all candidate-selection policies. Within the matched three-candidate budget, VoDER-Cal reduces held-out off-target deviation from 0.344 under target-only selection to 0.276 and improves listener-rated preservation. Preservation-sensitive evaluation therefore complements prompt-adherence evaluation, while candidate reranking offers a practical inference-time improvement. Code, configuration files, and analysis scripts are available at https://github.com/intelland/VoDER

</details>


### [111] [AnyBand: Unified Multi-Bandwidth Speech Extension via Frequency-Aware In-Context Spectral Infilling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00572)
*Junchuan Zhao,Minh Duc Vu,Bowen Zhang,Ye Wang*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: AnyBand提出了一种统一的带宽扩展（BWE）框架，将带宽扩展重新定义为上下文频谱填充（in-context spectral infilling）。它将可获得的低频段视为频率域提示（prompt），通过缺失频带条件流匹配（missing-band conditional flow matching）和Easy-to-Balanced截止频率课程学习，让单一模型支持连续范围内任意输入带宽的截止频率条件生成。实验表明，AnyBand在多种数据集和带宽设置下优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BWE方法通常将带宽扩展视为固定或预定义的带宽转换问题，当输入带宽变化时需要特定截止频率的模型或重新训练，限制了实际场景中语音可能以不同截止频率到达的适用性。

Method: 1) 将BWE重构为上下文频谱填充，利用可用频带作为频率域提示；2) 采用缺失频带条件流匹配训练；3) 引入Easy-to-Balanced截止频率课程在连续采样截止频率上训练；4) 设计频率感知的Diffusion Transformer建模跨频率交互和长时程依赖；5) 采用物理启发的多视角对抗精炼阶段增强频谱真实性、包络一致性和谐波一致性。

Result: 在多个数据集和带宽设置下，AnyBand在频谱重建上持续优于现有基线，并在标准和不规则输入截止频率下均达到具有竞争力的感知质量。

Conclusion: AnyBand通过上下文频谱填充的范式，实现了单一模型对连续范围输入带宽的统一BWE处理，验证了该框架在实际多样化截止频率场景下的有效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Bandwidth extension (BWE) aims to recover missing high-frequency content from band-limited speech. Existing methods often formulate BWE as a fixed or predefined bandwidth conversion problem, potentially requiring cutoff-specific models or retraining when the input bandwidth changes. This assumption limits their applicability to practical scenarios where speech may arrive with diverse cutoff frequencies. We propose AnyBand, a unified BWE framework that recasts bandwidth extension as in-context spectral infilling. Motivated by prompt-based zero-shot speech generation, AnyBand conditions high-frequency generation on the observed low-frequency spectrum, using the available band as a frequency-domain prompt that conveys content, speaker, prosodic, and spectral-envelope cues. This formulation enables a single model to perform cutoff-conditioned generation over a continuous range of input bandwidths. AnyBand is trained with missing-band conditional flow matching and an Easy-to-Balanced cutoff curriculum over continuously sampled cutoff frequencies. To better exploit the spectral prompt, we introduce a frequency-aware Diffusion Transformer that models cross-frequency interactions and long-range temporal dependencies, followed by a physically motivated multi-view adversarial refinement stage to enhance spectral realism, envelope coherence, and harmonic consistency. Experiments on multiple datasets and bandwidth settings show that AnyBand consistently improves spectral reconstruction over existing baselines while achieving competitive perceptual quality across both standard and irregular input cutoffs. Audio samples are available.

</details>


### [112] [UOT-IR: Structured Routing of High-Polyphony Symbolic Music into Fixed-Budget Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00576)
*Ziyue Kang,Nan Nan,Chenhao Lin,Xiaohong Guan*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出UOT-IR，一种基于约束非平衡最优传输的训练无关框架，用于将高复音音乐符号乐谱在固定预算（固定轨道/槽位）下压缩为紧凑且有界表示，在模板标准化和自适应保留两种设定下均取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高复音符号音乐在生成、分析和编曲中日益普遍，但许多下游任务需要固定轨道或槽位的有界表示。现有方法依赖启发式简化或通用表示空间降维，往往无法在严格预算下保持结构角色、配器兼容性和可演奏性。

Method: 将压缩问题重新表述为固定预算的结构化路由问题，提出基于约束非平衡最优传输的训练无关框架UOT-IR。该框架结合配器先验、自适应边际松弛、时间解码和可演奏性感知投影，生成紧凑且音乐连贯的有界表示。涵盖两种设定：模板标准化（映射到预定义有界模板）和自适应保留（无外部模板下保留代表性内容）。

Result: 在SymphonyNet语料库上，UOT-IR在两种设定下均表现优异：自适应保留设定下最佳Note-F1达0.9120；模板标准化设定下结构损失最低（14.7165）、坏结构混淆率最低（0.3406）。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了固定预算符号音乐压缩的原则性范式，为获得紧凑、结构化且音乐连贯的符号表示提供了实践路径。

Abstract: High-polyphony symbolic music is increasingly used in generation, analysis, and arrangement, yet many downstream tasks require bounded representations with fixed tracks or slots. Converting richly orchestrated scores into compact forms is therefore necessary, but existing approaches relying on heuristic simplification or generic representation-space reduction often fail to preserve structural roles, orchestration compatibility, and playability under strict budgets. To address the issue, this study reformulates the compression problem as a fixed-budget structured routing problem and proposes Unbalanced Optimal Transport for Information Routing (UOT-IR), a training-free framework based on constrained unbalanced optimal transport. UOT-IR combines an orchestration prior, adaptive marginal relaxation, temporal decoding, and playability-aware projection to produce compact and musically coherent bounded representations. This work further studies two practical settings under the same slot budget: template standardization, which maps each input to a predefined bounded template, and adaptive preservation, which retains representative content without assuming an external template. Experiments on the SymphonyNet corpus show that UOT-IR delivers strong overall performance across both settings, including the best Note-F1 in adaptive preservation (0.9120), together with the lowest structural cost (14.7165) and bad structural confusion rate (0.3406) in template standardization. This work establishes a principled paradigm for fixed-budget symbolic music compression, offering a practical path toward compact, structured, and musically coherent symbolic representations.

</details>


### [113] [SoniSpeech: A Large-Scale Open-Vocabulary Tri-Modal Dataset for Wearable Silent Speech Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00803)
*Ruidong Zhang,Jiacheng Liu,François Guimbretière,Cheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: SoniSpeech是首个大规模、开放词表的可穿戴无声语音接口三模态数据集，基于声学感应眼镜收集了34小时、18,000条语音，包含超声回波轮廓、发声音频和正面视频三种同步模态，覆盖5,356个独特单词和完整音素覆盖。基于CTC的ResNet-34基线在开放词表无声语音识别上达到26.3%的词错误率，为该任务提供了首个基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可穿戴无声语音接口（SSI）受限于小规模、封闭词汇表；而能实现较大词汇量的方法需要面部电极等笨重的硬件设备，缺乏一种既能实现开放词表识别又保持可穿戴便利性的解决方案。

Method: 构建了一个三模态同步数据集，使用声学感应眼镜采集超声回波轮廓、发声音频和正面视频三种模态，数据来自SODA对话数据集以保证当代对话英语的多样性；采用CTC损失函数训练的ResNet-34作为基线模型进行无声语音识别。

Result: 数据集包含34小时、18,000条语音，5,356个独特单词及完整音素覆盖；基线模型在开放词表无声语音识别任务上取得26.3%的词错误率，建立了该任务的第一个基准。

Conclusion: SoniSpeech填补了可穿戴无声语音接口领域缺乏大规模开放词表数据集的空白，证明了基于声学感应眼镜的超声信号在开放词表无声语音识别中的可行性，为后续研究提供了基准和公开数据资源。

Abstract: Wearable silent speech interfaces (SSIs) are limited to small, closed vocabularies. Approaches achieving larger vocabularies require obtrusive hardware such as facial electrodes. We present SoniSpeech, the first large-scale, open-vocabulary, trimodal dataset for wearable SSI using acoustic-sensing eyewear. It contains 34 hours across 18,000 utterances with three synchronized modalities: ultrasound echo profiles, voiced audio, and frontal video, in both voiced and silent modes. The corpus draws from the SODA dialogue dataset, providing contemporary conversational English with 5,356 unique words and full phoneme coverage. A CTC-based ResNet-34 baseline achieves 26.3% word error rate (WER) on open-vocabulary silent speech recognition, the first benchmark for this task. Dataset is available at https://doi.org/10.7298/xjjr-9m85

</details>


### [114] [DRONEAUDIONET: Noise Suppression for Drone Audition-based Search and Rescue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00875)
*Chitralekha Gupta,Soundarya Ramesh,Yifei Luo,Suranga Nanayakkara*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出DRONEAUDIONET，一种针对无人机（UAV）旋翼噪声抑制的方法。该方法将源分离模型重新框架为无人机噪声估计器，通过引入可学习的掩码缩放机制（允许掩码幅度超过1）和加性残差校正项，在信噪比远低于-10 dB的无人机主导混合信号中实现有效的噪声抑制和声源恢复。在公开数据集上的实验表明，该方法能持续提升下游声音分类性能，尤其对人类声音的增益最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机搭载麦克风可实现空中声学场景分析（如搜救、野生动物监测、工业检测），但旋翼噪声在混合信号中占据主导地位（SNR远低于-10 dB），而现有的增强和源分离方法大多针对近平衡混合信号设计，在无人机听觉场景下性能显著退化。因此需要针对无人机噪声特点进行专门建模。

Method: 将源分离模型重新构建为无人机噪声估计器。具体包括两个关键设计：(1) 可学习的掩码缩放机制，允许掩码幅度超过1，以更好地建模无人机主导的混合信号；(2) 加性残差校正项，用于改进无人机噪声估计和声源恢复。模型在公开的无人机听觉数据集上训练和评估，并在包含未见过的无人机硬件和飞行模式的外部数据集上测试泛化能力。

Result: DRONEAUDIONET在多个指标上持续改善下游声音分类性能，其中对人类声音（如呼救声）的增益最为显著。在包含未见无人机硬件和飞行模式的域外数据集上也表现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 针对无人机的特定噪声建模对于稳健的空中声学感知至关重要，源分离方法在真实世界的无人机辅助搜救等应用中具有巨大潜力。

Abstract: Microphones mounted on UAVs enable aerial acoustic scene analysis applications such as search-and-rescue, wildlife monitoring, and industrial inspection. However, drone rotor noise often dominates the mixture signal at SNRs well below -10 dB, making source recovery extremely challenging. Existing enhancement and source separation methods are typically designed for near-balanced mixtures and degrade substantially in drone audition settings. In this work, we propose DRONEAUDIONET, a drone noise suppression method that reframes a source separation model as a drone noise estimator. To better model drone-dominant mixtures, we introduce a learnable mask-scaling mechanism that allows mask magnitudes beyond unity, together with an additive residual correction term for improved drone estimation and source recovery. We train and evaluate our model on a publicly available drone audition dataset and test generalizability on an out-of-domain dataset with unseen drone hardware and flight modes. Results show that DRONEAUDIONET consistently improves downstream sound classification performance, with the largest gains observed for human vocal sounds. Our findings demonstrate the importance of drone-specific modeling for robust aerial acoustic perception and highlight the potential of source separation methods for real-world drone-assisted search-and-rescue.

</details>


### [115] [Separate-and-Detect: Unified Drum Transcription and Stem Generation via Latent Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01093)
*Wei-Han Hsu,Chih-Cheng Chang,Bo-Yu Chen,Li Su,Yi-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"先分离后检测"的自动鼓转录（ADT）框架，利用五音轨潜在扩散模型将鼓声源分离为可编辑音频音轨，再用固定起始点检测器将各音轨转化为符号事件。训练阶段引入两个辅助分支（起始点分支和音色分支）塑造分离器，推理时丢弃。在MDB Drums和ENST-Drums数据集上，该管线在转录F1上优于基于U-Net的分离基线和代表性端到端ADT系统，同时提供分离音频音轨。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ADT通常将音乐混合直接映射到符号鼓事件，虽有效但丢弃了对编辑、混音和制作有用的声学音轨。作者希望通过分离与检测的替代方案，在转录的同时提供可编辑的鼓音轨。

Method: 采用"分离-检测"两阶段管线：前端的五音轨源分离器基于潜在扩散模型，在紧凑VAE潜在空间中联合生成底鼓、军鼓、通鼓、踩镲和镲片五个鼓音轨；后端的固定起始点检测器将每个音轨转换为符号事件。训练时引入两个仅训练期使用的辅助分支——起始点分支（OB）和音色分支（TB），用于塑造分离器学习但推理时丢弃。

Result: 在合成鼓多音轨上训练，在MDB Drums和ENST-Drums上评测，该管线在整体转录F1上一致优于强U-Net鼓分离基线，并在底鼓和军鼓F1上优于代表性端到端ADT系统。消融显示OB带来最稳定的转录提升，TB则改变重建、音轨质量和起始点检测之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 生成式鼓源分离不仅能作为源分离模型，还能作为可解释鼓转录的实用前端，兼顾转录准确性与可编辑音频音轨的输出能力。

Abstract: Automatic Drum Transcription (ADT) is commonly formulated as a direct mapping from a music mixture to symbolic drum events. While effective for transcription, this formulation discards the acoustic stems that are useful for editing, remixing, and production. We revisit an alternative separate-and-detect formulation, where a drum source separation front end first produces five editable drum stems, and a fixed onset detector then converts each stem into symbolic events. The separator is built on a five-stem latent diffusion model that jointly generates kick, snare, toms, hi-hats, and cymbals in a compact VAE latent space. We further study two training-only auxiliary branches--an onset branch (OB) and a timbre branch (TB)--which shape the separator during learning but are discarded at inference. Trained on synthetic drum multitracks and evaluated on MDB Drums and ENST-Drums, the proposed pipeline consistently improves over a strong U-Net-based drum separation baseline in overall transcription F1. It also outperforms a representative end-to-end ADT system on kick and snare F1 under our evaluation protocol, while additionally providing separated audio stems. The ablation results show that OB gives the most stable transcription gains, whereas TB changes the trade-off between reconstruction, acoustic stem quality, and onset detection. These results suggest that generative drum demixing can serve not only as a source separation model, but also as a practical front end for interpretable drum transcription.

</details>


### [116] [JoyAI-Talker: Full-Duplex Speech Interactive Large Model Built for Empathetic Voice Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01119)
*Yinhao Bai,Jinming Chen,Yafeng Chen,Wei Deng,Boya Dong,Nan Duan,Yu Gu,Weisheng Han,Yankun Huang,Ming Ke,Hao Li,Jingdong Li,Xiangyu Liang,Ning Liu,Yuan Liu,Ji Miao,Jiaqi Wang,Qi Wang,Wenchao Wang,Yuxuan Wang,Zhenfang Wang,Zhangyu Xiao,Chao Xue,Hongfei Xue,Fan Yu,Tianyi Zhang,Yuan Zhang,Yuqi Zhang,Lin Zhu*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: JoyAI-Talker是一个全双工语音对话系统，采用模块化Thinker-Talker架构，通过统一语音-文本联合训练缓解"认知退化"问题，并引入PAER框架（人格自适应共情响应）与Joy-Duplex全双工框架，在保持基础模型推理能力的同时实现富有表现力和共情力的语音交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音对话系统在扩展语音交互能力时往往导致基础模型的文本推理、STEM和逻辑能力退化（即"认知退化"瓶颈），同时缺乏对富有表现力语音合成、共情响应和自然全双工交互的支持。作者旨在解决这些关键瓶颈，构建既能保持强大认知能力又能实现自然共情对话的语音系统。

Method: 1) 模块化Thinker-Talker架构：分离认知推理与语音合成功能；2) 统一语音-文本联合训练管道：缓解认知退化，保留核心文本能力并扩展到语音交互；3) Talker模块采用文本可控生成范式，通过自然语言指令灵活控制声音属性和局部副语言事件（如笑声、叹气）；4) PAER框架：通过分层认知管道从原始音频提取非语言说话人线索（性别、年龄、情绪），融入Thinker的思维链推理，生成语义与表现力对齐的上下文自适应响应；5) Joy-Duplex：基于状态驱动的即插即用全双工框架，作为实时轮次控制的门控引擎。

Result: 在T2T和S2T基准测试上达到高度竞争性的性能；在全双工评估中，用户打断场景下响应率达到0.88，同时在背景语音下保持极低的误触发率，证明系统支持流畅自然的语音对话。

Conclusion: JoyAI-Talker通过创新架构设计和多层次的框架整合，成功解决了全双工语音对话系统在认知能力保持、表达力控制和共情交互方面的关键挑战，展示了其在实时、自然、富有情感交互的语音对话场景中的实用性和高效性。

Abstract: We present JoyAI-Talker, a full-duplex speech dialogue system that delivers robust foundation model capabilities while empowering empathetic interaction and voice agent intelligence. JoyAI-Talker adopts a modular Thinker-Talker architecture and further implements a unified speech-text joint training pipeline to mitigate the common "cognitive degradation" bottleneck, thereby largely preserving the model's core textual reasoning, STEM, and logical capabilities while extending them to speech-based interaction. For expressive speech synthesis, the Talker module employs a text-controllable generation paradigm that enables natural-language instructions to flexibly control vocal attributes and localized paralinguistic events, such as laughter and sighs, supporting more expressive and fine-grained speech responses. To enhance conversational empathy, we introduce the Persona-Adaptive Empathetic Response (PAER) framework. PAER employs a hierarchical cognitive pipeline to extract non-verbal speaker cues, such as gender, age, and emotional state, from raw input audio, incorporate them into the Thinker's CoT reasoning, and generate context-adaptive responses that align semantically appropriate text with fine-grained control over utterance-level expressiveness and localized paralinguistic events, including sighs, speaking rate, and volume. We further integrate Joy-Duplex, a state-driven, plug-and-play full-duplex framework that functions as an efficient gating engine for real-time turn control. Extensive evaluations show that JoyAI-Talker achieves highly competitive performance on foundational T2T and S2T benchmarks. In full-duplex evaluation, the system reaches a high response rate of 0.88 under user interruptions while maintaining an extremely low false-trigger rate under background speech, demonstrating its readiness for fluid and natural speech dialogue.

</details>


### [117] [Comparative Validation of GPT-4o-mini and Teacher Mean Scores for Automated Scoring of Music Analysis Responses: Single-Pass Deployment, Repeatability, and Strategy-Specific Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01783)
*Baicheng Lin,Lingxi Jin,Kyung-Seok Min*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本研究评估了GPT-4o-mini在基于量规的音乐分析论文评分中的有效性与可重复性，以教师平均分为基准。300篇大学水平学生答卷在四个维度（和声、形式、推理、术语）上被评分，使用三种提示策略（少样本+思维链、检索增强生成、自一致性），每种策略实施三次。结果显示Fs+CoT与教师平均分的吻合度最强，RAG存在系统性过度评分，SC可重复性高但个体层面一致性较弱；术语维度的评分一致性普遍弱于推理维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式音乐分析应答的评分耗时且需要对和声知识与形式理解做出细致判断。研究旨在验证GPT-4o-mini能否作为辅助评分工具，为此需要严格评估其在复杂音乐分析任务上的有效性和可重复性。

Method: 使用300篇大学水平的学生应答数据集，由教师从和声、形式、推理、术语四个维度评分。GPT-4o-mini采用三种提示策略进行评分：少样本+思维链（Fs+CoT）、检索增强生成（RAG）、以及基于五次内部生成的自一致性（SC）。每种策略重复三次，比较单次评分与中位数聚合结果。使用相关性、组内相关系数、Krippendorff's alpha、二次加权kappa及评分误差指数评估与教师平均分的一致性。

Result: Fs+CoT在单次评分和聚合中均与教师平均分表现出最强的一致性。RAG呈现系统性过度评分；SC产生高度可重复但个体层面一致性较弱的评分。维度分析显示评分在量规组件间存在差异，术语维度的评分一致性普遍弱于推理维度。

Conclusion: GPT-4o-mini能够为复杂的音乐分析应答生成稳定的评分，但不同的提示策略产生不同的评分特征。实际应用中需要针对具体策略进行校准、维度级验证，并保持人工监督。

Abstract: Scoring open-ended music analysis responses is time-consuming and requires nuanced judgments of harmonic knowledge and formal understanding. This study evaluates the validity and repeatability of GPT-4o-mini for rubric-based scoring of music analysis essays, using teacher mean scores as the benchmark. A dataset of 300 university-level student responses was scored by teachers on four dimensions: Harmony, Form, Reasoning, and Terminology. GPT-4o-mini scored the same responses using three prompting strategies: few-shot prompting with chain-of-thought reasoning (Fs+CoT), retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), and self-consistency based on five internal generations per administration (SC). Each strategy was administered three times with the model, prompt, rubric, and response held constant. Single-pass scores represented an operational scoring condition, whereas median aggregation across three runs was used to examine robustness. Agreement with teacher mean scores was evaluated using correlation, intraclass correlation, Krippendorff's alpha, quadratic weighted kappa, and scoring error indices. Fs+CoT showed the strongest agreement with teacher mean scores in both single-pass scoring and median aggregation. RAG showed systematic over-scoring, whereas SC produced highly repeatable scores but weaker individual-level agreement. Dimension-level analyses showed that scoring performance varied across rubric components, with Terminology generally showing weaker agreement than Reasoning. These findings indicate that GPT-4o-mini can generate stable scores for complex music analysis responses, but prompting strategies produce distinct scoring profiles. Operational use therefore requires strategy-specific calibration, dimension-level validation, and continued human oversight.

</details>


### [118] [Multi-Backbone Self-Supervised Ensembles for Audio Deepfake Detection and a Cross-Track Analysis of Generation-Detection Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01796)
*Seunghyun Kim,Junghyun Kim,Jiyoung Woo*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文介绍了"Go-To-Germany"团队在ImageCLEF 2026音频深度伪造检测与生成任务中的参与情况。检测系统采用四骨干自监督学习（SSL）集成方案（WavLM-Large、Wav2Vec2-XLS-R-300M、ECAPA-TDNN和x-vector），在官方评测中获得0.9522的最终得分；生成子任务中，官方提交的F5-TTS v1基线在带混响处理后排名第一。论文通过跨赛道分析揭示了检测与生成之间的显著不对称性，并提出多骨干SSL集成的"架构保险"假说。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在参与ImageCLEF 2026音频深度伪造检测与生成挑战赛，验证多骨干自监督学习集成在音频深度伪造检测中的有效性，同时通过生成子任务的反取证探测来探究检测与生成两侧之间的内在联系，识别部署中的核心挑战。

Method: 检测侧采用四骨干SSL集成模型（WavLM-Large、Wav2Vec2-XLS-R-300M、ECAPA-TDNN、x-vector）；生成侧提出四模型方案（GLM-TTS、F5-TTS、XTTS v2、CosyVoice3），官方提交采用F5-TTS v1基线加均匀混响处理作为反取证探测。分析方法包括LOSO 56说话人交叉验证、三区域骨干几何分析、bootstrap置信区间、PCA分析和五项预注册的证伪实验。

Result: 检测系统在官方评测中取得0.9522分，对参与者生成的深度伪造准确率达100%，对留出组织者真实数据准确率为0.8875（存在11.25%假阳性差距）。生成子任务以F5-TTS v1+混响方案排名第一（最终得分0.4304，WER 4.99%，CER 2.07%），成功规避61.4%的参与者检测器和56.2%的组织者检测器。跨赛道分析揭示检测（0.9522）与生成（0.4304）最终得分之间的显著不对称性。

Conclusion: 多骨干SSL集成展现出强大的深度伪造检测能力，但也暴露了对真实数据11.25%假阳性率的部署瓶颈。生成侧的反取证方案虽排名第一但得分远低于检测侧，说明当前系统"防伪"能力强于"造假"能力。作者通过证伪实验支持了"架构保险"假说，并指出消除假阳性差距是未来部署的首要挑战。

Abstract: This paper describes the participation of team "Go-To-Germany" in the ImageCLEF 2026 Audio Deepfake Detection and Generation task. Our detection system, built on a four-backbone self-supervised learning (SSL) ensemble combining WavLM-Large, Wav2Vec2-XLS-R-300M, ECAPA-TDNN, and x-vector representations, achieved a final score of 0.9522 on the official ImageCLEF 2026 evaluation, with perfect accuracy (1.0000) on participant-generated deepfakes and 0.8875 on the held-out organizer ground-truth real data. For the Generation sub-task, our official team submission, an F5-TTS v1 baseline processed with a uniform reverberation pass and submitted as a deliberate anti-forensic probe, ranked first with a final score of 0.4304 (word error rate (WER) 4.99%, character error rate (CER) 2.07%); details of our four-model program (GLM-TTS, F5-TTS, XTTS v2, CosyVoice3), from which the official entry was drawn, appear in the paper. We present a cross-track analysis revealing a pronounced asymmetry: our detection system identifies 100% of participant-generated deepfakes, while our official generation entry, despite ranking first in the Audio Generation sub-task and evading 61.4% and 56.2% of participant and organizer detectors, attains a Final Score of 0.4304 against 0.9522 on the Detection side. We further report falsification-based ablation experiments (LOSO 56-speaker cross-validation, three-region backbone geometry, bootstrap confidence intervals, and PCA analysis) that motivate our architectural-insurance hypothesis for multi-backbone SSL ensembling. We complement these results with five cross-track insights and five pre-registered falsification experiments connecting generation-side evasion to detection-side design decisions, and we openly report an 11.25% false-positive gap on held-out organizer real recordings as the principal open challenge for deployment.

</details>


### [119] [P-MUSE: Prompt-MIDI-Optional Model for Unified Instrumental Music Synthesis and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01920)
*Chong Jing,Junan Zhang,Jing Yang,Yulun Wu,Fan Fan,Zhizheng Wu*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: P-MUSE是一套统一的器乐MIDI-to-Music框架，通过多阶段课程学习将"仅提示音频的条件生成"与"提示音频+MIDI配对的情境学习"两种范式统一，支持可选的提示MIDI输入，并统一了音乐生成与局部编辑任务。文章还提出阶段感知无分类器引导调度原则和Tail-Drop策略，并建立了首个覆盖多种提示模式、生成/编辑任务及四种乐器的综合基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MIDI-to-Music系统分为两种范式：仅依赖提示音频的条件生成（适用于无对齐提示MIDI的情况）与基于提示音频和MIDI配对的情境学习（利用跨模态对齐获得更强的MIDI跟随和音色相似性控制）。这两种范式彼此分离，缺乏统一的框架，且生成与编辑任务在现有系统中也未统一处理。

Method: 提出P-MUSE框架，通过多阶段课程学习实现提示MIDI可选的统一输入（prompt-MIDI-optional），将条件生成与情境学习两种范式融合；并通过共享的fill-in-the-middle（FIM）公式统一音乐生成与局部编辑。基于理论分析和实证研究，提出阶段感知无分类器引导调度原则（phase-aware classifier-free guidance scheduling）以及Tail-Drop策略。

Result: 建立了首个全面基准，覆盖不同提示模式、生成/编辑任务以及钢琴、吉他、贝斯和鼓四种代表性乐器，并在该基准上验证了P-MUSE的统一框架与提出的引导调度和Tail-Drop策略的有效性。

Conclusion: P-MUSE成功统一了MIDI-to-Music的两种主流范式以及生成与编辑任务，为转录到音频系统提出了可行的引导调度原则和采样优化策略，并提供了标准化基准以推动该领域的研究进展。

Abstract: MIDI-to-Music system renders the melody and rhythm of a target MIDI sequence into musical segment while cloning instrument timbre from a prompt recording. Existing systems typically adopt one of two distinct paradigms: conditional generation with prompt audio alone, which remains applicable when aligned prompt MIDI is unavailable, and In-Context Learning with paired prompt audio and MIDI, which exploits cross-modal alignment for stronger control on MIDI following and timbre similarity. We introduce P-MUSE, an instrumental MIDI-to-Music framework that unifies both paradigms via a multi-stage Curriculum-Learning supporting prompt-MIDI-optional inputs. P-MUSE further unifies music generation and local editing through a shared fill-in-the-middle formulation. Grounded in theoretical analysis and empirical study, we propose a phase-aware classifier-free guidance scheduling principle for Transcription-to-Audio systems, alongside a Tail-Drop strategy. Finally, to advance research in this field, we establish the first comprehensive benchmark, covering various prompt modes, generation/editing tasks, and four representative instruments: piano, guitar, bass, and drums. Demos are available at https://p-muse.github.io/.

</details>


### [120] [Uncertainty-Aware Crossmodal Fusion for Classification of Animal Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02104)
*Ehsan Yaghoubi,Florian Haselbeck*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出不确定性感知融合（UAF）框架，通过双流架构分别从原始波形和log-Mel频谱图中提取特征，并利用高斯不确定性加权融合两种声学表征，以应对真实环境下动物发声分类中的噪声、混响和传感器退化等挑战。在跨物种、身份级别的评估中，UAF在17类猪发声基准和3类犬吠数据集上分别达到59.4%/39.7%和73.1%/71.5%的准确率/macro F1，显著优于静态拼接融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实环境下的动物声学监测面临环境噪声、混响、重叠叫声和传感器退化等非受控条件，使自动化分类困难。现有的两种主流声学表征（原始波形和log-Mel频谱图）各有互补性局限：原始波形保留时间微结构但受裁剪和混响影响，log-Mel频谱图捕获谐波组织但丢失相位信息且对宽带噪声敏感。需要一种能融合两种表征优势并动态权衡其可靠性的方法。

Method: 提出不确定性感知融合（UAF）框架，采用双流架构：一个流处理原始波形，另一个流处理log-Mel频谱图，每个流对自身表征估计高斯不确定性（均值与方差），通过不确定性加权实现自适应融合，赋予更自信的表征更高权重，且无需任何可靠性标签。

Result: 在排除训练中见过的所有个体的跨物种、身份级别评估中，UAF（平均池化）在17类SoundWel猪发声基准上达到59.4%准确率/39.7% macro F1，在3类DogBark数据集上达到73.1%准确率/71.5% macro F1，相较静态拼接融合分别相对提升15.7%和20.4%的macro F1。通过四种时序聚合策略的消融实验表明，不确定性融合而非动物叫声的时序特征才是性能提升的主要驱动力。

Conclusion: 不确定性感知融合框架通过自适应加权融合原始波形和log-Mel频谱图两种互补表征，无需可靠性标签即可有效应对真实环境下的声学退化，为动物发声自动分类提供了一种鲁棒且可推广的解决方案。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence offers substantial potential for acoustic monitoring of animals, from welfare assessment in precision livestock farming to wildlife conservation and ecological research, where vocalizations can indicate health, stress, and social states earlier and at lower cost than manual observation. However, recordings in these settings are obtained under uncontrolled conditions, including environmental noise, reverberation, overlapping calls, and sensors that degrade without notice. As a consequence, automated classification of animal vocalizations remains challenging, and the two dominant acoustic representations show complementary limitations: raw waveforms preserve temporal microstructure but degrade under clipping and reverberation, while log-Mel spectrograms capture harmonic organization but lose phase information and are sensitive to broadband noise. To address these challenges, we propose Uncertainty-Aware Fusion (UAF), a dual-stream framework that estimates Gaussian uncertainty for each representation and fuses them via uncertainty weighting. This mechanism assigns greater weight to the more confident representation with no reliability labels required. In a cross-species, identity-based evaluation excluding all individuals seen during training, UAF (mean pooling) achieves 59.4\% accuracy / 39.7\% macro F1 on the 17-class SoundWel pig vocalization benchmark and 73.1\% accuracy / 71.5\% macro F1 on the 3-class DogBark dataset, outperforming static-concatenation fusion by 15.7\% and 20.4\% relative macro F1, respectively. Ablations over four temporal aggregation strategies show that uncertainty fusion, rather than the temporal characteristics of animal calls, is the primary driver of the performance gain.

</details>


### [121] [Sounding Canvas: Embedding Algorithms in Networked, Sensorial Sound Art](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02219)
*Luciano Ciamarone,Dora Motèque,Marco Giordano*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: Sounding Canvas 是一个将绘画转变为触控响应式多模态装置的作品，通过在画布内嵌入电容传感器、实时决策模型和网络功能，使触摸触发从画作本身发出的空间化声音。系统结合基于 CNN 的离线视觉到声音映射以定义声音词汇，以及两个在线事件管理器（高阶马尔可夫模型和基于 LSTM 的策略）来平衡响应性与引导式探索，从而探讨算法在嵌入式计算艺术中的可感知性、协作创作以及作者身份问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在探索如何将算法以多维度方式嵌入艺术作品——不仅是物理层面的感知与计算隐藏于画作背后，还包括感知层面通过离线视觉-声音映射将绘画特征与声音描述符对齐，以及在表演层面通过在线模型塑造与观众和远程画布的实时互动，从而提出关于嵌入式算法艺术中作者身份、代理权和评估的问题。

Method: 系统结合了CNN-based离线映射来定义画作的声音词汇，并以两个在线事件管理器驱动实时交互：一个高阶马尔可夫模型和一个基于LSTM的策略，二者协同在响应性与引导式探索之间取得平衡。硬件层面在画布中嵌入电容传感器与网络通信，使触摸触发空间化声音并支持跨网络远程画布协作。

Result: 该作品成功实现了算法通过行为而非代码被感知的目标；网络功能将孤立的触摸转化为分布式协作创作；同时展示了嵌入式算法艺术中算法层如何同时作用于物理嵌入、感知映射和表演交互三个层面。

Conclusion: Sounding Canvas 表明算法可以同时被物理嵌入、感知映射和表演性地呈现在艺术作品中，其中离线映射定义创作词汇，在线模型塑造实时交互，网络功能则使个人触摸转变为协作行为。该作品为核心问题——如何让算法可感知、如何分配创作代理权以及如何评估嵌入式算法艺术——提供了实践性探索。

Abstract: Sounding Canvas turns painting into a touch-responsive multimodal installation by embedding capacitive sensors, real-time decision models, and networking inside the canvas. Touches trigger spatialised sounds that appear to emanate from the painting itself. The work embeds algorithms physically, as sensing and computation concealed behind the artwork; perceptually, through an offline visual-to-sonic mapping that aligns a painting's features with sound descriptors; and performatively, through online models that shape live interaction with visitors and with remote canvases over a network. We describe the artistic rationale and technical implementation, combining a CNN-based offline mapping that defines the sound vocabulary with two online event managers, a higher-order Markov model and an LSTM-based policy, that balance responsiveness with guided exploration. We discuss how these layers make algorithms perceptible through behaviour rather than code, how networking transforms solitary touch into distributed co-authorship, and how the system raises questions of authorship, agency, and evaluation in embedded algorithmic artworks.

</details>


### [122] [An End-to-End Workflow for Fin Whale Song Detection, Note Characterization, and Localization with Distributed Acoustic Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02387)
*Dídac Diego-Tortosa,Miriam Romagosa,Arantza Ugalde,Hugo Latorre,Sergi Ventosa,Jose Enrique García,Antonio Villaseñor*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于海底光纤分布式声学传感（DAS）的端到端工作流，用于长须鲸叫声的检测、表征和定位，在直布罗陀海峡和西阿尔沃兰海的两条海底电信电缆上进行了测试，取得了高精度检测结果，并支持叫声分离、类型区分和声源移动推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用海底光纤DAS对长须鲸进行大规模监测是一种有效途径，但缺乏从密集DAS记录中自动提取生物声学信息的系统性工作流，需要一套完整的检测、表征和定位方法。

Method: 该工作流包括：(1) 采用适应窄带长须鲸叫声的峰度值拾取器进行逐通道检测；(2) 基于密度的时空聚类将逐通道检测分组为个体叫声，结合聚类凝聚和双曲线拟合剔除不连贯的拾取；(3) 通过时间、频谱和能量相关描述符对保留的聚类进行表征，支持叫声类型判别和叫声间隔估计；(4) 利用跨DAS通道的相对到达时间进行网格搜索，估计候选声源位置。

Result: 在六段长须鲸歌声上，拾取级中位精确率为0.990、召回率为0.744；聚类级中位精确率为0.880、召回率为0.806。代表性应用展示了重叠发声的分离、A型和B型叫声的表征以及表观声源移动的推断。

Conclusion: 该工作流将密集DAS记录转化为紧凑的叫声级生物声学信息，为长须鲸监测提供了集成框架，并可作为其他同步声学接收器阵列适应的基础。

Abstract: Submarine fiber-optic cables instrumented with distributed acoustic sensing (DAS) provide an effective approach for large-scale monitoring of fin whales. We present an end-to-end workflow for detecting, characterizing, and localizing fin whale notes, tested on two submarine telecom cables in the Strait of Gibraltar and western Alboran Sea. The workflow applies a kurtosis-value picker adapted to narrow-band fin whale notes. Channel-wise detections are grouped into individual notes using density-based spatio-temporal clustering, cluster agglomeration, and hyperbolic fitting to reject incoherent picks. The retained clusters are characterized through temporal, spectral, and energy-related descriptors that support note-type discrimination and estimation of inter-note intervals. Relative arrival times across DAS channels are then used in a grid-search procedure to estimate candidate source locations. Evaluation against manually annotated detections from six fin whale songs yielded median pick-level precision of 0.990 and recall of 0.744, and median cluster-level precision of 0.880 and recall of 0.806. Representative applications demonstrate separation of overlapping vocalizations, characterization of type-A and type-B notes, and the inference of apparent source movement. By transforming dense DAS recordings into compact note-level bioacoustic information, the workflow provides an integrated framework for fin whale monitoring and a basis for adaptation to other synchronized acoustic receiver arrays.

</details>


### [123] [Can Foundation Models Hear What Made That Sound? A Tiered Benchmark of Audio-Language Models and Traditional Classifiers for Closed-Set Sound Source Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02397)
*Sajjad Abdoli,Ghassan Al-Sumaidaee,Ahmad ElShiekh,Ahmed Rashad*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文对11种音频分类方法进行基准测试，包括任务感知的闭集LLM（Gemini系列和Kimi-Audio）、固定词汇标签器（YAMNet, PANNs, Whisper-AT, SSLAM）、零样本音频-文本模型（CLAP）和音频基础LLM（BAT），在包含2,242个片段、23个细粒度类别和11个类别的闭集声源识别任务上评估。方法被分为四个评估层级，报告宏精度、召回率、F1和假阴性率。Gemini-3.1-Pro-Preview表现最佳（类别级F1 85.6%，细粒度F1 56.7%），SSLAM和CLAP在类别级匹配或超过最佳闭集模型。通过对8,968条Gemini链式思维的分析，发现响应长度不能预测准确性，错误答案的置信度高达92-100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频分类方法种类繁多，包括闭集LLM、固定词汇标签器、零样本音频-文本模型等，它们在任务接收方式和输出评分机制上存在根本差异，缺乏统一的公平基准评测。此外，现有工作很少深入分析这些模型的推理过程和错误模式，尤其是在LLM链式思维与准确性之间的关系方面。

Method: 将11种音频分类方法按任务接收方式和输出评分机制分为四个评估层级：(1)任务感知闭集LLM（四种Gemini模型加Kimi-Audio-7B-Instruct）；(2)固定词汇标签器（YAMNet, PANNs, Whisper-AT, SSLAM）；(3)零样本音频-文本模型（CLAP）；(4)音频基础LLM（BAT）。在2,242个片段、23个细粒度类别、11个类别的闭集声源识别任务上统一评估，分别报告宏精度、召回率、F1和假阴性率，并提供每类混淆矩阵。

Result: Gemini-3.1-Pro-Preview最优，达到85.6%类别级F1和56.7%细粒度F1。Kimi-Audio达到67.5%类别级F1和32.9%细粒度F1，但有1.6%样本无法回答。SSLAM和CLAP在类别级匹配或超过最佳闭集模型，但细粒度级落后。Gemini模型8,968条链式思维分析表明：响应长度与准确性无关；"整体判断优于详细分析"的表观效应实为难度混淆；错误答案有92-100%的时间以高置信度呈现。

Conclusion: 不同方法家族在音频分类任务中各有优劣：闭集LLM在细粒度识别上具优势，但标签器和零样本模型在类别级仍有竞争力。LLM的链式思维长度不能作为准确性的可靠指标，且高置信度错误是系统性风险。作者提供了各粒度的完整混淆矩阵和结构化错误模式分析，为不同应用场景下的方法选择给出实用建议。

Abstract: We benchmark eleven audio classification methods: five task-aware closed-set LLMs (four Gemini models plus open-weight Kimi-Audio-7B-Instruct), four fixed-vocabulary taggers (YAMNet, PANNs, Whisper-AT, and SSLAM), a zero-shot audio-text model (CLAP), and an audio-grounded LLM (BAT). We evaluate them on a closed-set sound-source identification task over 2,242 clips spanning 23 fine-grained classes and 11 categories. Since these methods differ fundamentally in how they receive the task and how outputs are scored, we group them into four evaluation tiers rather than one leaderboard, reporting macro Precision, Recall, F1, and false-negative rate per tier. The best model, Gemini-3.1-Pro-Preview, reaches 85.6 percent category-level F1 and 56.7 percent fine-grained F1. Kimi-Audio is competitive for its size, reaching 67.5 percent category-level F1 and 32.9 percent fine-grained F1, but fails to answer 1.6 percent of samples. SSLAM and CLAP match or exceed the best closed-set model at the category level without seeing the candidate list, but fall behind at the fine-grained level. Analyzing the Gemini models' chain-of-thought across 8,968 responses, we find that response length does not predict accuracy, an apparent "holistic judgment beats detailed analysis" effect is better explained as a difficulty confound, and wrong answers are stated confidently 92 to 100 percent of the time. We report full per-class confusion matrices and metrics for all eleven methods, identify the structural error modes behind most of the accuracy loss between granularities, and give practical guidance for choosing among these method families.

</details>


<div id='eess.AS'></div>

# eess.AS [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Anomalous Sound Detection Meets Noise-Aware Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00447)
*Takuya Fujimura,Gordon Wichern,Yoshiki Masuyama,Christoph Boeddeker,Kohei Saijo,Julius Richter,Takahiro Edo,Jonathan Le Roux*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出噪声感知自监督学习（NA-SSL）模型用于噪声感知异常声音检测（NA-ASD）任务，利用双通道录音中的远麦克风噪声信号作为辅助信息，提取近麦克风信号的干净自监督表示，并在DCASE 2026 Challenge Task 2开发集上验证了有效性，其中NA-BEATs系统以70.24%的官方分数大幅领先第二名（65.46%）赢得挑战赛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统异常声音检测（ASD）在工业场景中常受背景噪声干扰，单一麦克风难以分离目标机器声音与噪声。NA-ASD任务通过双通道录音（近麦克风采集目标机器信号、远麦克风采集背景噪声）来解决这一问题，但如何有效利用噪声信息提升ASD表现仍是一个挑战。

Method: 利用多种音频数据集模拟双通道录音，训练NA-SSL模型以提取近麦克风信号的干净自监督表示，将远麦克风的噪声主导录音作为辅助信息；训练完成的NA-SSL模型作为前端接入标准ASD框架，并在三种基础SSL模型（BEATs、EAT和Dasheng）上验证，同时比较有/无判别性微调两种设置。

Result: 在DCASE 2026 Challenge Task 2开发集上，NA-SSL框架在三种基础SSL模型上均表现出有效性；在挑战赛中，NA-BEATs系统以70.24%的官方成绩获胜，比第二名（65.46%）高出显著差距。

Conclusion: 噪声感知自监督学习框架能有效利用远麦克风的背景噪声信息提升异常声音检测性能，且具有良好的泛化性（适用于多种基础SSL模型），在DCASE 2026挑战赛中取得了领先水平的结果。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce noise-aware self-supervised learning (NA-SSL) models for noise-aware anomalous sound detection (NA-ASD). NA-ASD is an ASD task with two-channel audio recordings, where one microphone is located close to the target machine and the other is located farther away to capture noise. For this task, we simulate two-channel recordings using diverse audio datasets and train NA-SSL models to extract clean SSL representations of the close-microphone signal by using the far-microphone recording dominated by background noise as auxiliary information. The NA-SSL models are then used as frontends in the standard ASD framework. Our experimental evaluation on the DCASE 2026 Challenge Task 2 development dataset demonstrates the effectiveness of the NA-SSL framework across three base SSL models (BEATs, EAT, and Dasheng), both with and without discriminative fine-tuning. Furthermore, the challenge results proved the effectiveness of the proposed approach, where the NA-BEATs system won the challenge by a large margin, achieving an official score of 70.24%, while the second-place system achieved 65.46%.

</details>


### [125] [Simulation-Based Plate-Reverb Parameter Estimation from a Single Impulse Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00656)
*Minhui Lu,Joshua D. Reiss*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于仿真训练的非迭代估计器，用于第1届DAFx参数估计挑战赛任务A。该方法通过幅度、频谱和衰减描述符汇总非归一化板混响冲激响应，并使用树回归器集成一次性估计六个目标参数。在两组独立合成验证集上，归一化模型优于训练集均值和早期原始回归基线；在共享数据集上，最终集成模型以更低推理成本优于单次官方默认PSO。由于官方标签隐藏，参数精度在模拟器匹配数据上评估，结果仅支持音频侧一致性检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决板混响参数估计问题，挑战在于官方标签不可得、传统迭代优化方法（如PSO）推理成本高，需要一种高效且准确的非迭代估计方法。

Method: 采用仿真训练的非迭代估计器：提取每个未归一化板混响冲激响应的幅度、频谱和衰减描述符作为特征，使用树回归器集成（ensemble of tree regressors）在一次前向传播中估计六个目标参数。

Result: 在两组独立合成验证集上，归一化模型优于训练集均值和早期原始回归基线；在共享数据集上，最终集成的性能优于单次运行的官方默认PSO，且推理成本显著更低。

Conclusion: 仿真训练的非迭代估计器在板混响参数估计中具有高效性和良好精度，能以更低计算成本超越传统迭代优化方法；但受限于官方标签隐藏，评估仅能在模拟器匹配数据上进行，且输出为点估计、不提供不确定性。

Abstract: We present a simulation-trained, non-iterative estimator for Task A of the 1st DAFx Parameter Estimation Challenge. Each unnormalized plate-reverb impulse response is summarized by amplitude, spectral, and decay descriptors, and an ensemble of tree regressors estimates the six target parameters in one pass. Across two independent synthetic validation sets, the normalized models outperform the training-set mean and an earlier raw-regression baseline. On a shared set, the final ensemble also outperforms a single run of the official default PSO at substantially lower inference cost. Since the official labels are hidden, parameter accuracy is measured on simulator-matched data, and the released responses support only audio-side consistency checks. The estimator returns point estimates without uncertainty.

</details>


### [126] [Band-Count Dense Modal Estimation with Fixed-Frequency Differentiable Resonator Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00667)
*Minhui Lu,Joshua D. Reiss*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于ExtraTrees回归器与可微全极点谐振器模型相结合的方法，用于DAFx参数估计挑战赛Task B中密集板式混响脉冲响应的模态参数估计，相比官方基线将误差降低约66%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集板式混响脉冲响应中模态弱且重叠，传统稀疏峰值检测方法容易严重低估模态数量，导致参数估计不准确。需要一种更可靠的模态数量估计方法。

Method: 首先在合成器生成的数据上训练ExtraTrees回归器，预测四个频率频段中的模态数量；这些数量定义密集频率网格；随后使用可微全极点谐振器模型在固定频率的情况下细化衰减率和增益参数。

Result: 在两个独立的合成验证集上，该方法相比官方默认峰值拾取基线，将局部挑战风格误差降低了约66%。改进主要归因于模态数量失配的减少，而衰减率和增益仍是最大的误差来源。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持将模态密度估计与连续参数拟合分离的做法，即先估计模态数量/频率位置，再拟合其余连续参数，这一策略在密集模态场景下是有效的。

Abstract: Task B of the 1st DAFx Parameter Estimation Challenge requires estimating the frequencies, decay rates, gains, and number of modes in a dense plate-reverb impulse response. Weak and overlapping modes make sparse peak detection prone to severe undercounting. We train an ExtraTrees regressor on simulator-generated data to predict mode counts in four frequency bands. These counts define dense frequency grids, after which a differentiable all-pole resonator model refines decay and gain while keeping frequency fixed. On two separate synthetic validation sets, the system reduces a local challenge-style error by about 66% relative to the official default peak-picking baseline. The improvement is mainly associated with lower mode-count mismatch, while decay and gain remain the largest error sources. These findings support separating modal-density estimation from continuous parameter fitting.

</details>


### [127] [Experience-Calibrated Contrastive Decoding for Mitigating Hallucinations in LM-Based Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00722)
*Chenlin Liu,Minghui Fang,Zhonghao Bi,Zekai Su,Rong Wang,Jiqing Han*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出基于经验校准的对比解码方法（ECCD），用于缓解基于语言模型的语音合成（LM-based TTS）中的语音幻觉问题。这是一个无需训练的解码时控制方法，通过对比有无文本条件的语音LM预测来增强对齐信息同时保留经验信息。实验表明ECCD在四个模型上大幅降低WER/CER（最高55.6%），听力测试CMOS增益+0.644。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于语言模型的TTS系统在生成语音时容易产生偏离目标文本的语音幻觉。现有缓解方法主要依赖架构改动或额外训练，而解码时控制这一方向尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出条件信息视角，区分文本派生对齐信息与声学上下文及习得语音规律提供的经验信息。提出ECCD方法：（1）使用同一语音LM在有无文本条件下的预测进行对比解码；（2）保留原始专家分布，仅施加正向对齐增强；（3）利用集合级经验兼容性校准增强强度，无需训练。

Result: 在四个模型上，ECCD在全部SeedTTS-Eval设置中降低WER/CER最高达55.6%，在24/25的多语言CV3-Eval设置中也有效。听力测试CMOS增益为+0.644，同时保持较强的说话人相似度。进一步分析表明对齐影响和决策级增益在语言单元内存在变化，首错边界处低于匹配的正确边界。

Conclusion: 条件信息控制被证明是缓解语音幻觉的一个有前景的解码时方向，为LM-based TTS的幻觉缓解提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Language model-based text-to-speech (LM-based TTS) remains vulnerable to speech hallucinations that deviate from the target text. Existing mitigation mainly relies on architectural changes or additional training, while decoding-time control remains underexplored. We present a conditional information view that distinguishes text-derived alignment information from experience information supplied by acoustic context and learned speech regularities. We hypothesize that an important class of hallucinations begins when alignment support is insufficiently reflected in the selected token at a vulnerable transition. Using predictions from the same speech LM with and without text conditions, we propose Experience-Calibrated Contrastive Decoding (ECCD), a training-free method that strengthens alignment support while preserving useful experience information. ECCD preserves the original expert distribution, applies only positive alignment enhancement, and calibrates its strength using set-level experience compatibility. Across four models, ECCD reduces WER/CER by up to 55.6% in all SeedTTS-Eval settings and 24 of 25 multilingual CV3-Eval settings. A listening test yields a CMOS gain of $+0.644$ while retaining strong speaker similarity. Further analysis shows that alignment influence and decision-level gain vary within linguistic units and are lower at first-error boundaries than at matched correct boundaries. Overall, these extensive experiments and analyses identify conditional information control as a promising decoding-time direction for mitigating speech hallucination.

</details>


### [128] [REIMU: Efficient Heterogeneous Hierarchical Reasoning for SSL-Based Speech Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00857)
*Kwok-Ho Ng,Tingting Song,Bingwen Feng,Peiya Li*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了递归层次推理（REIMU）在语音深度伪造检测中的实用有效性，比较了传统单遍骨干网络、权重共享递归、同构HRM和异构HRM四种架构在四种Base规模的SSL前端上的表现。结果表明递归和层次分解本身并不能带来性能提升，而异构算子分配（自注意力与线性注意力结合）提供了更具竞争力的配置，且在下游参数减少10.8%的情况下仍保持竞争力，展示了参数高效语音深度伪造检测的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到语音和语音转换系统生成的语音越来越逼真，对媒体完整性和语音认证构成日益严重的挑战。自监督学习（SSL）虽然显著推进了语音深度伪造检测，但下游骨干网络通常仅通过单次前向传播处理SSL表示，尚未系统探索递归推理和层次分解在该任务中的实际效果。

Method: 提出受控研究REIMU，系统比较四种下游架构：传统单遍骨干网络、权重共享递归、同构HRM（同质层次推理模块）以及异构HRM（异质层次推理模块）。在四种Base规模的SSL前端上进行评估，并在异构HRM中结合自注意力和线性注意力的高低层次模块。

Result: 在ASVspoof 2019和2021评测集上的实验表明，递归和层次分解并不固有地提升检测性能，而异构算子分配提供了更具竞争力的配置。异构设计在比匹配基线减少10.8%下游参数的情况下仍保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 递归和层次分解并非语音深度伪造检测性能提升的关键因素，异构算子分配是更具前景的方向，为实现参数高效的语音深度伪造检测提供了新思路。

Abstract: The increasing realism of speech generated by text-to-speech and voice conversion systems poses growing challenges to media integrity and voice authentication. Self-supervised learning (SSL) has substantially advanced speech deepfake detection, where downstream backbones conventionally process SSL representations through a single forward pass. This work investigates the practical effectiveness of recurrent hierarchical reasoning for this task. We term this controlled study REIMU and systematically compare conventional single-pass backbones, weight-shared recurrence, homogeneous HRM, and heterogeneous HRM across four Base-scale SSL frontends. We further examine heterogeneous high- and low-level modules that combine self-attention with linear attention. Experiments on the ASVspoof 2019 and 2021 evaluation sets show that recurrence and hierarchical decomposition do not inherently improve detection, whereas heterogeneous operator assignment provides a more competitive configuration. Notably, the heterogeneous design remains competitive while using 10.8\% fewer downstream parameters than the matched baseline, demonstrating its potential for parameter-efficient speech deepfake detection.

</details>


### [129] [Beyond One-Size-Fits-All: Personalized and Culturally Adaptive Emotional TTS via Interactive Optimization of Individual Emotion Perception Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00998)
*Wangzixi Zhou,Bagus Tris Atmaja,Sakriani Sakti*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出一种个性化和文化自适应的情感语音合成（TTS）框架，通过交互式遗传算法对个体化的唤醒-效价（A-V）感知空间进行迭代优化，使合成语音的情感表达更贴合每位听众的感知，超越传统使用平均A-V值的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感TTS系统大多依赖离散情感标签，难以捕捉人类情感的细腻变化；虽然维度模型（如Russell的A-V模型）提供了更精细的控制，但情感感知具有个体和文化差异，使用平均化A-V值会导致模型化情感与实际感知情感不匹配。

Method: 提出一个个性化和文化自适应的情感TTS框架，核心使用交互式遗传算法对个体化的A-V感知空间进行迭代优化，根据每位听众的反馈逐步调整情感表征，使其更贴合个体感知。

Result: 在日语、中文和印尼语参与者上的评估表明，相比使用平均A-V值的模型，该框架生成的语音具有更符合听众感知的情感表达，验证了个性化和文化适应的重要性。

Conclusion: 个性化与文化适应是超越"一刀切"情感TTS的关键方向，通过交互优化个体感知空间可显著提升情感表达与听众感知的对齐程度。

Abstract: The rise of conversational AI has increased interest in emotional Text-to-Speech (TTS). Most systems rely on discrete emotion labels, which fail to capture the nuanced nature of human affect. Recent models employ dimensional representations such as Russell's arousal-valence (A-V) model, offering finer control. However, emotional perception varies across individuals and cultures, which may cause mismatches between modeled and perceived emotions. We propose a personalized and culturally adaptive emotional TTS framework that performs interactive optimization of individualized A-V perception spaces using an Interactive Genetic Algorithm. By adapting emotion representations to each listener, the system produces speech with more perceptually aligned emotional expression than models using averaged A-V values. Evaluations with Japanese, Chinese, and Indonesian participants highlight the importance of personalization and cultural adaptation for moving beyond one-size-fits-all emotional TTS.

</details>


### [130] [Latent Softmax for Data-Efficient Phoneme-Based Multilingual ASR Across Tonal and Non-Tonal Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01281)
*Saierdaer Yusuyin,Nanling Jiang,Hao Huang,Zhijian Ou*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出Latent Softmax，一种CTC兼容的输出层，用于多语言ASR中处理声调语言（带声调标注的元音）与非声调语言（仅基础元音标签）监督粒度不匹配的问题。通过将带声调元音建模为子类、基础元音建模为主类，并在只有主类标签时将子类视为潜在变量进行边缘化，在AISHELL-1和LibriSpeech多语言实验中显著降低了音素错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于音素的多语言ASR在联合训练声调语言（如中文）和非声调语言（如英语）时，监督粒度不匹配：声调语言标注带声调的元音，而非声调语言只提供基础元音标签。标准softmax要么将两者视为不相关的类别削弱跨语言共享，要么折叠声调损失声调语言所需的区分能力。急需一种既能跨语言共享又保留声调区分度的输出层设计。

Method: 提出Latent Softmax：一种CTC兼容的输出层，将带声调元音作为子类（subclass），基础元音作为主类（major class），辅音和CTC blank保持单例标签。当观测到基础元音主类标签时，带声调元音子类被视为潜在变量并被边缘化（marginalized out）。该设计使得多语言CTC训练中声调与非声调语言的监督信号能够对齐。

Result: 在AISHELL-1（普通话）和LibriSpeech（英语）多语言实验中，Latent Softmax相比标准softmax多语言基线，将语音到音素（S2P）音素错误率在AISHELL-1上降低8.4%，在LibriSpeech test-clean上降低17.5%，test-other上降低12.6%。改进的语音到音素编码器在LLM音素到字形转换和基于projector的接口上均带来一致的词错误率改善。经过代码切换适应后，Latent Softmax进一步在ASRU2019上将projector-based混合错误率降低2.6%，在CS-Dialogue上降低9.5%。

Conclusion: Latent Softmax通过子类-主类层次建模有效解决了多语言ASR中声调与非声调语言监督粒度不匹配的问题，既保持了声调语言的区分能力，又增强了跨语言声学证据共享，在音素识别和下游词错误率上均取得显著改进。

Abstract: Phoneme-based multilingual automatic speech recognition (ASR) can share acoustic evidence across languages more directly than language-specific subword modeling. When tonal and non-tonal languages are jointly trained, however, their supervision granularity does not match: tonal languages annotate tone-marked vowels, whereas non-tonal languages typically provide only base-vowel labels. A standard softmax either treats the two as unrelated classes, weakening cross-lingual sharing, or collapses tones, losing distinctions required by tonal languages. We propose Latent Softmax, a connectionist temporal classification (CTC)-compatible output layer that models tone-marked vowels as subclasses and base vowels as major classes, while consonants and the CTC blank remain singleton labels. When only a base-vowel major-class label is observed, the tone-marked vowel subclass is treated as latent and marginalized out. Multilingual experiments on AISHELL-1 Mandarin and LibriSpeech English show that Latent Softmax reduces speech-to-phoneme (S2P) phoneme error rates over a standard softmax multilingual baseline by 8.4% on AISHELL-1, 17.5% on LibriSpeech test-clean, and 12.6% on test-other. The improved speech-to-phoneme encoders also yield consistent word error rate gains for both large-language-model phoneme-to-grapheme conversion and projector-based interfaces. After code-switching adaptation, Latent Softmax further reduces projector-based mixed error rate by 2.6% on ASRU2019 and 9.5% on CS-Dialogue datasets.

</details>


### [131] [Music Restoration via Latent Operator Optimization and Diffusion Model Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01972)
*Michal Švento,Eloi Moliner,Valtteri Kallinen,Lauri Juvela,Vesa Välimäki,Pavel Rajmic*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: LOUDAR是一个通用的音频修复方法，在预训练音频自编码器的隐空间中运行，将未知失真建模为可学习的隐空间算子，推理时交替估计干净隐变量和更新算子参数，并通过无条件的隐扩散模型作为先验来约束估计，适用于多种音频修复任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频修复系统依赖成对训练数据和失真特定的监督信号，当前向退化过程未知时无法使用，缺乏通用的、不依赖失真先验知识的修复方法。

Method: LOUDAR在预训练音频自编码器的隐空间中工作，将未知失真建模为可学习的隐空间算子；推理时交替估计干净隐变量和更新算子参数；使用无条件的隐扩散模型作为干净音频的先验，将隐变量估计引导向干净录音的流形。退化模型针对每个输入自适应调整。

Result: 在人声效果移除与修复以及吉他失真去除任务上评估，LOUDAR一致性地优于退化输入，并与监督和无监督基线在波形域和隐域上具有竞争力。

Conclusion: LOUDAR通过隐空间优化和扩散先验实现了无需预知失真类型的通用音频修复，在多种修复问题上具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: Music restoration seeks to recover a clean signal from an observed recording degraded by an unknown effect, distortion, or corruption. Existing systems often rely on paired training data and distortion-specific supervision, which limits their use when the forward process is not known in advance. We propose LOUDAR (Latent-space Optimization of Unknown Distortion for Audio Restoration) a general-purpose restoration method that operates in the latent space of a pretrained audio autoencoder and models the unknown distortion as a learnable latent operator. At inference time, LOUDAR alternates between estimating the clean latent variable and updating the latent operator parameters. An unconditional latent diffusion model provides a prior over clean audio and regularizes this inference by steering the latent estimate toward the manifold of clean recordings. Because the degradation model is adapted per input, the approach is broadly applicable across diverse restoration problems. We evaluate LOUDAR on singing voice effect removal and restoration, as well as guitar distortion removal, and show that it consistently improves over degraded inputs and is competitive with supervised and unsupervised baselines in waveform and latent domains.

</details>


### [132] [SwanTale: Unified Multi-Speaker Speech and Audio Generation for Instruct and Zero-Shot Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02023)
*Yu Zhang,Ruiqi Li,Changhao Pan,Ke Lei,Xiang Yin,Cheng Yang*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出SwanTale，一个支持零样本和指令控制的多说话人表达性语音与音频生成模型，通过构建SwanData-Caption数据集（清洗原始数据、添加合成覆盖、标注多级字幕）和SwanVAE（支持多音频模态的高质量生成），结合奖励条件质量控制、Engram条件化、统一MoE、课程学习与GRPO后训练，在多项零样本和指令控制指标上取得领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动画配音、有声剧、电影、广告、游戏、播客和短视频等场景中，创作者需要无需参考录音即可设计声音、用自然语言控制说话人风格、支持带环境和音效的声学场景，并能复用已设计的音色。现有方法难以同时支持指令任务（基于字幕生成语音和音频）和零样本任务（基于参考音频生成），因此需要从数据和模型两方面同时解决这些任务。

Method: 1) 数据侧：提出SwanData-Caption，清洗原始语音和音频数据，添加有针对性的合成覆盖，并标注多样且准确的多级字幕；2) 模型侧：提出SwanTale，包含SwanVAE以支持高质量多音频模态生成，采用奖励条件质量控制、Engram条件化、统一MoE实现多任务多模态建模，并使用课程学习和GRPO后训练让模型渐进学习并强化能力。

Result: SwanTale在多项零样本和指令控制关键指标上取得领先，在两任务中均获得最佳表达性评分，并支持涉及多说话人语音和音频的复杂指令生成。

Conclusion: SwanTale通过数据构建（SwanData-Caption）和模型设计（SwanVAE、MoE、课程学习、GRPO）的结合，有效解决了多说话人语音和音频生成中零样本与指令控制任务的关键挑战，实验验证了其优越性和实用性。

Abstract: Speech and audio generation is often needed in animation dubbing, audio drama, movies, advertising, games, podcasts, and short-video production. In these scenarios, creators may need to design voices without reference recordings, control speaker styles with natural language, support acoustic scenes with environments and audio effects, and later reuse the designed voices. Therefore, it is important to support multi-speaker speech and audio generation for both instruct and zero-shot tasks. The instruct task requires a caption of the environment, speaker styles, and fine-grained content, while the zero-shot task uses reference audio together with the same fine-grained content. We address these tasks from both the data and model sides. First, we propose SwanData-Caption, which cleans raw speech and audio data, adds targeted synthetic coverage, and annotates diverse and accurate multi-level captions. Then, we propose SwanTale, a multi-speaker expressive speech and audio generation model that supports both zero-shot and instruct tasks. We introduce SwanVAE to support high-quality multi-audio-modality generation. Then, we adopt reward-conditioned quality control and Engram conditioning, along with Unified MoE for multi-task and multi-audio-modality modeling. In addition, we use curriculum learning and GRPO post-training to let the model progressively learn and strengthen its capabilities. Experimental results show that SwanTale leads on multiple key zero-shot and instruct metrics, achieves the best expressiveness scores in both tasks, and supports complex instruct generation involving multi-speaker speech and audio. Demos can be found at https://swanaigc.github.io/\#swantale.

</details>


### [133] [MEMS Microphones as Ultrasonic Transducers: Nonlinear Electrostatic Actuation and a Parametric Array Prototype](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02203)
*Xiaoyu Niu,Zihuan Liu,Ehsan Vatankhah,Yuqi Meng,Neal A. Hall*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文研究将商用电容式MEMS麦克风芯片用作空气耦合超声发射器，利用非线性吸合和回弹驱动方式实现大位移振动，并构建了紧凑的参数阵列原型。28芯片阵列在83和93 kHz驱动下产生10 kHz差频方向性声束，并与解析辐射理论和有限元建模进行了对比验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统空气耦合超声发射器通常体积大、成本高且难以小型化。作者希望探索利用现有商用MEMS麦克风芯片的电容结构，通过非线性拉进（pull-in）和回弹（snap-back）驱动机制产生大幅振动，从而实现低成本、紧凑型的参数阵列超声发射方案。

Method: 采用商用电容式MEMS麦克风芯片作为超声发射单元，利用非线性吸合与回弹驱动模式获得大位移输出。构建了包含28个芯片的阵列，以83 kHz和93 kHz双频率驱动，利用参量阵效应产生10 kHz差频信号。实验测量与解析辐射理论及有限元建模结果进行对比，并分析孔径、填充因子、器件均匀性和接收器非线性等因素的影响。

Result: 单个芯片即能产生可测量的空气超声压力和大膜片位移；28芯片阵列在双频驱动下成功产生10 kHz差频方向性声束。测量结果与解析理论和有限元仿真基本吻合，验证了该方案的可行性与理论模型的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究表明商用MEMS麦克风芯片在非线性驱动下可作为高效、紧凑的空气耦合超声参量阵发射器，为小型化超声设备和声学探测应用提供了新的低成本技术路径。孔径、填充因子、器件一致性及接收端非线性是影响系统性能的关键设计因素。

Abstract: This paper investigates commercial-style capacitive MEMS microphone dies as air-coupled ultrasonic transmitters under nonlinear pull-in and snap-back actuation and demonstrates a compact parametric-array prototype. A single die produces large diaphragm displacement and measurable ultrasonic pressure in air. A 28-die array driven at 83 and 93 kHz generates a directional component at the 10 kHz difference frequency. Measurements are compared with analytical radiation theory and finite-element modeling, and the effects of aperture, fill factor, device uniformity, and receiver nonlinearity are discussed.

</details>


### [134] [Deep Learning-Based Active Trim Panels for Enhanced Aircraft Interior Noise Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02421)
*Boxiang Wang,Malte Misol,Zhengding Luo,Junwei Ji,Xiaoyi Shen,Dongyuan Shi,Woon-Seng Gan*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出了一种温度感知的选择性固定滤波器主动噪声控制（TP-SFANC）方法，利用轻量级一维卷积神经网络（1D CNN）结合多任务学习策略，同时处理参考信号和误差信号，学习频率与温度特征以动态选择最优控制滤波器，从而在衬里温度变化下有效抑制多音调噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机舱主动噪声控制（ANC）装饰板用于抑制多音调噪声，传统的选择性固定滤波器ANC（SFANC）方法在频率变化时表现良好，但真实工况中衬里温度变化会改变声学与结构路径，导致降噪性能退化。因此需要一种能感知温度变化并保持降噪性能的方法。

Method: 提出温度感知SFANC（TP-SFANC）方案，采用轻量级1D卷积神经网络（1D CNN），通过多任务学习策略训练，同时处理参考信号和误差信号，学习频率和温度特征，从而动态选择最优的控制滤波器。

Result: 数值仿真结果表明，所提出的方法在不同频率和衬里温度条件下能够有效衰减多音调噪声。

Conclusion: TP-SFANC方法通过引入温度感知能力，解决了温度变化条件下SFANC降噪性能退化的问题，在变化的频率和衬里温度下均能实现有效的多音调噪声抑制。

Abstract: Active noise control (ANC) trim panels offer an effective solution to suppress multi-tonal noise in aircraft. The selective fixed-filter ANC (SFANC) method, characterized by low computational complexity, high robustness and rapid response, is suitable to handle multi-tonal engine noise that varies in frequency due to changes in the rotational speed of the engine shaft. However, real-world conditions introduce variations in lining temperature, altering acoustic and structural paths and degrading noise reduction performance. To address this challenge, a temperature-perceptive SFANC (TP-SFANC) approach is proposed that employs a lightweight one-dimensional convolutional neural network (1D CNN) trained using a multi-task learning strategy. By processing both reference and error signals, the 1D CNN learns frequency and temperature characteristics to dynamically select the optimal control filter. Numerical simulations demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method in attenuating multi-tonal noise across varying frequencies and lining temperatures.

</details>


<div id='nlin.SI'></div>

# nlin.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [Ermakov-Painleve' II Symmetry Reduction of a Class of Moving Boundary Problems for a Reciprocal Extended mKdV Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00681)
*Colin Rogers,Sandra Carillo*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 本文通过互逆变换将一个经典孤子mKdV方程的可积扩展与一个含源项的新型非线性演化方程联系起来，并利用Ermakov-Painlevé II对称性约简求解了一类Stefan型移动边界问题的精确解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究可积系统之间的变换关系以及利用对称性约简方法解决带有移动边界的物理问题，特别是将孤子方程的可积结构与Stefan型问题联系起来，以获得精确解析解。

Method: 首先应用互逆变换建立mKdV方程可积扩展与含源非线性演化方程之间的等价联系；随后采用Ermakov-Painlevé II对称性约简方法对相关问题进行归约，从而求解Stefan型移动边界问题的精确解。

Result: 成功将可积mKdV扩展映射为含源的新型非线性演化方程，并通过Ermakov-Painlevé II对称性约简获得了Stefan型移动边界问题的一类精确解析解。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了互逆变换在连接不同类型非线性方程方面的有效性，同时Ermakov-Painlevé II对称性约简为求解含移动边界的非线性问题提供了一个强有力的解析工具，拓展了可积系统理论在相变和边界问题中的应用。

Abstract: A reciprocal transformation is applied to link an integrable extension of the classical solitonic mKdV to a novel nonlinear evolution equation incorporating a source term. Application of Ermakov-Painleve' II symmetry reduction is made to determine the exact solution to a class of associated moving boundary problems of Stefan-type.

</details>


### [136] [Deformation algorithm: Deforming (2+1)-dimensional integrable systems to higher dimensional ones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01293)
*Wang Fa-Ren,Jia Man,Lou S Y*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 本文建立了两个(2+1)维可积方程（各向异性KP方程和各向同性NNV方程）的统一守恒律变形框架，通过引入一组互相对易的场依赖变形算子，构造无穷维($m+3$)维可积KP和NNV层级及其闭式Lax对，并通过截断得到(3+1)维KP系统和(4+1)维广义NNV系统。对称性约化不仅恢复了原始(2+1)维方程，还产生了两种新的Harry-Dym型互反可积系统，其中各向同性(2+1)维HD系统为首个报道，填补了文献空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于守恒律的变形算法已成功将(1+1)维可积系统提升到高维并保持Lax可积性，但将其推广到(2+1)维模型一直是一个未解决的开放问题。作者旨在建立适用于(2+1)维可积方程的统一变形框架，以扩展守恒律变形猜想并填补高维可积系统构造的空白。

Method: 该方法引入一组互相对易的场依赖变形算子，系统构造($m+3$)维可积KP和NNV层级；推导各层级的闭式Lax对，并通过Lax算子的对易子消失条件严格验证可积性；通过截断辅助空间变量获得有限维主系统；进一步通过对称性约化恢复原始方程并生成Harry-Dym型互反可积系统。

Result: 成功构造了($m+3$)维KP和NNV层级及其Lax对；获得(3+1)维KP系统和(4+1)维广义NNV系统；对称性约化产生两种HD型互反可积系统，包括首个各向同性二维空间HD系统。KP和NNV分支的对比表明原始二维母方程的空间对称性直接决定其高维变形和HD对偶子系统的对称性质。

Conclusion: 该工作将守恒律变形猜想从(1+1)维扩展到(2+1)维，同时兼容强Lax和弱Lax结构，为多维可积系统构建互反联系提供了普适路径，填补了各向同性二维空间HD系统的文献空白。

Abstract: The deformation algorithm based on conservation laws can lift (1+1)-dimensional integrable systems to higher-dimensional counterparts while preserving Lax integrability, yet its generalization to (2+1)-dimensional models has remained an open problem. This paper establishes a unified deformation framework for two (2+1)-dimensional integrable equations: the anisotropic Kadomtsev-Petviashvili (KP) equation and the isotropic Nizhnik-Novikov-Veselov (NNV) equation. By introducing a set of mutually commuting field-dependent deformation operators, we systematically construct infinite families of ($m+3$)-dimensional integrable KP and NNV hierarchies, derive their closed-form Lax pairs, and rigorously verify integrability via the vanishing commutator condition of Lax operators. For each high-dimensional hierarchy, concrete finite-dimensional master systems are obtained by truncating auxiliary spatial variables: a (3+1)-dimensional KP system and a (4+1)-dimensional generalized NNV system. Further symmetry reductions recover the original (2+1)-dimensional KP and NNV equations, and more importantly produce two distinct Harry-Dym (HD)-type reciprocal integrable systems. The KP reduction yields an anisotropic (2+1)-dimensional HD model, while the NNV reduction generates the first spatially isotropic two-space-dimensional HD system reported so far, filling a notable gap in existing literature. Parallel comparison of the KP and NNV branches reveals that the spatial symmetry of the original two-dimensional parent equation directly governs the symmetry properties of its high-dimensional deformations and HD dual subsystems. Our work not only extends the conservation-law deformation conjecture beyond (1+1)-dimensions to accommodate both strong Lax and weak Lax structures, but also provides a universal route to construct reciprocal links for multi-dimensional integrable systems.

</details>
