### [137] [Anomalous enhancement of thermal radiation transport by quasidisorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00384)
*Cheng-Long Zhou,Xin-Yu Jia,Shui-Hua Yang,Yan Wang,Yong Zhang,Hong-Liang Yi,Mauro Antezza*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现从有序到无序的转变在近场热辐射中反而能增强能量传输：打破长程有序（从周期到准周期构型）会诱导逆直觉的输运增强，准周期元素间的离域相互作用可在远距离上中继并放大热电磁能量传输，超越相应的近场场景，为热流操控提供新机遇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为从有序到无序的转变（order-to-disorder）不利于固态传热，经典波和准粒子系统中这一认知已被广泛接受。然而，这一范式是否适用于近场热辐射尚不清楚，研究者希望探索打破长程有序能否在近场热辐射中产生全新的输运行为。

Method: 从周期性构型转向准周期性（quasiperiodic）构型，在近场热辐射框架下分析受此"准无序效应"（quasidisorder effect）影响的能量传输机制，重点研究准周期元素间的离域相互作用及其对热电磁能量传输的放大与中继作用。

Result: 近场热辐射中打破长程有序（从周期到准周期）可逆直觉地增强能量传输，准周期元素间的离域相互作用能在跨越较大空间距离时中继并放大热电磁能量传输，其性能甚至超过相应的近场场景。

Conclusion: 准无序效应诱导的非常规近场热辐射输运特性为热流操控开启了新机遇，对热科学具有变革性意义，并推动了对非有序系统中集体激发的基础物理理解。

Abstract: The transition from order to disorder is conventionally regarded as detrimental to solid-state heat transfer in classical wave and quasiparticle systems. In striking contrast, we show that in near-field thermal radiation, breaking long-range order-shifting from periodic to quasiperiodic configurations-induces a counterintuitive enhancement of energy transport. This effect arises from delocalized interactions within quasiperiodic elements, where this quasiperiodicity relays and amplifies thermal electromagnetic energy transfer across large spatial separations, surpassing even corresponding near-field scenarios. The extraordinary transport properties induced by quasidisorder effect of near-field thermal radiation could unlock exciting opportunities for heat flow manipulation, offering transformative implications for thermal science and advancing the fundamental understanding of collective excitations in non-ordered systems.

</details>


### [138] [Topological Rainbow Trapping for Spatial-frequency Demultiplexing of Underwater Acoustic Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01108)
*Cheng Lin,Yangkai Liu,Tuo Liu,Yi Zhang,Haiyan Fan,Hui Zhang*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 该论文基于声学量子谷霍尔效应（QVHE）和量子自旋霍尔效应（QSHE）的类比，提出了两种水下空间-频率解复用机制，利用结构化表面声波（SSAW）实现多频声波的高效分离与定位。QVHE机制通过谷霍尔边缘通道中的空间梯度使不同频率分量在不同位置局域；QSHE机制则将一维拓扑边缘态与频率选择性的零维高阶角态耦合，实现"先传输后局域"的远程彩虹捕获。数值模拟与实验验证了频率相关局域化及缺陷条件下的传输路径鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下目标识别和声能收集需要高效的多频声波分离与定位，但水下拓扑彩虹捕获面临复杂流固耦合、开放系统中长程传输与频率选择性局域化难以集成的挑战。现有方法依赖封闭波导，难以在实际水下环境中应用。

Method: 论文提出两种机制：(1) QVHE机制：在谷霍尔边缘通道中引入空间梯度，使局部边缘态色散发生偏移，不同频率分量在不同位置局域化，实现频谱和空间解复用；(2) QSHE机制：将一维拓扑边缘态与频率选择性的零维高阶角态耦合，多频信号先沿共同边界鲁棒传播，再按频率转移到预设的远端角落，实现传输-捕获过程。两者均基于结构化表面声波（SSAW），其场被限制在结构化表面附近并向周围水体倏逝衰减，无需封闭波导。

Result: 数值模拟和实验结果验证了频率依赖的局域化行为，以及在存在结构缺陷时设计传输路径的持续性。两种机制均能在开放声表面波平台上实现鲁棒的物理层频率解复用。

Conclusion: 所提出的开放SSAW平台在物理层实现鲁棒的频率解复用，减少了对数字信号处理的依赖，为水下目标识别和频率选择性声能收集提供了潜在应用前景。

Abstract: Efficient separation and localization of multifrequency acoustic waves are essential for underwater target recognition and acoustic energy harvesting. The underwater implementation of topological rainbow trapping remains challenging because of complex fluid-solid interactions and the difficulty of integrating long-range transport with frequency-selective localization in an open system. Here, we theoretically develop and experimentally demonstrate two underwater spatial-frequency demultiplexing mechanisms based on the acoustic analogues of the QVHE and QSHE. Both mechanisms employ SSAWs, whose fields are confined near a structured surface and decay evanescently into the surrounding water, enabling experiments without an enclosed waveguide. In the QVHE mechanism, a spatial gradient along a valley-Hall edge channel shifts the local edge-state dispersion, causing different frequency components to become localized at distinct positions and thereby realizing spectral and spatial demultiplexing. In the QSHE mechanism, one-dimensional topological edge states are coupled to frequency-selective zero-dimensional higher-order corner states. Multifrequency signals first propagate robustly along a common boundary and are then transferred to prescribed remote corners according to frequency, producing a transport-then-confinement process. This mechanism combines defect-tolerant edge transport, frequency-selective corner localization, and remote rainbow trapping. Numerical simulations and experiments verify the frequency-dependent localization and the persistence of the designed transport pathways in the presence of structural defects. The proposed open SSAW platform performs robust frequency demultiplexing at the physical layer, reducing reliance on digital signal processing and offering potential for underwater target recognition and frequency-selective acoustic energy harvesting.

</details>


### [139] [Active Passivation Tunes Hotspot Locations in GaN Transistors with In Situ Thermal Mechanical Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01257)
*Yicheng Wei,Sihang Liu,Zimu Jiang,jinquan Zhang,Zifeng Huang,Han Yang,Yang He,Jin Wei,Zhe Cheng*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种主动钝化技术，通过调节GaN晶体管中有源钝化层长度来主动调控热点位置，使其从栅极边缘移动到漏极侧钝化层边缘，实现热点空间位置的可控迁移。结合微拉曼热成像和电-热-力多物理场仿真，揭示了电场峰值和焦耳热的空间位移是热点迁移的驱动机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有散热研究大多聚焦于工程化散热路径，而忽略了热源本身的固有行为，缺乏对热点位置进行主动调控的手段。

Method: 采用主动钝化技术，通过调整有源钝化层长度来调控热点位置；利用微拉曼热成像进行原位热-力可视化，并结合多物理场电-热-力耦合仿真进行验证；通过电学分析探究热点迁移的物理机制。

Result: 成功实现了热点从栅极边缘到漏极侧钝化层边缘的空间迁移，建立了热点位置与钝化层长度的一对一空间对应关系；确认了电场峰值和局部焦耳热的空间位移是热点迁移的驱动力。

Conclusion: 这种主动热源调控策略为功率电子器件的散热设计提供了关键的设计指导，开辟了从被动散热到主动热源管理的全新思路。

Abstract: Efficient thermal dissipation has become critical in emerging electronic devices. However, most existing studies have primarily focused on engineering heat dissipation pathways, largely overlooking the intrinsic behavior of the heat source itself. We demonstrate an active passivation technology that proactively tunes hotspot locations in GaN transistors. By adjusting the active passivation layer length, the hotspot is shifted from the gate edge to the drain-side AP edge, establishing a clear one-to-one spatial correlation. In-situ thermal-mechanical visualization via micro-Raman thermography, combined with multi-physics electro-thermal-mechanical simulations, directly captures the spatial redistribution of both temperature and thermal stress profiles. Electrical analysis confirms that this hotspot migration is driven by the spatial shift of the peak electric field and localized Joule heating. This proactive heat-source tuning strategy provides critical design guidelines for power electronics.

</details>


### [140] [Twistsonics: engineering acoustic topological textures in moire sound lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01278)
*Wen-Yu Wang,Shuai Liu,Xiang-Yuan Xu,Qing Tong,Hao Ge,Yijie Shen,Ming-Hui Lu*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种相位控制的声学超表面平台，通过扭曲两个声学斯格明子晶格构建莫尔超晶格，在声学粒子速度场中生成具有可控拓扑数和几何构型的声学斯格明子袋，并验证了其缺陷容忍性和拓扑稳定性，同时实现从斯格明子晶格到麦隆晶格的拓扑相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 莫尔超晶格由扭曲周期系统形成，是涌现拓扑现象的强大平台，但将其用于声波场中实空间拓扑织构的编程尚未被充分探索。作者旨在将莫尔物理引入声学领域，实现可重构的实空间拓扑织构。

Method: 在穿孔声学超表面上合成并叠加两个扭曲的斯格明子晶格，通过相位控制实现声学斯格明子袋的构建。通过调控扭转角和旋转中心来控制袋的尺度和构型，并引入受控缺陷来评估莫尔斯格明子袋的缺陷容忍性。同一平台还支持从斯格明子晶格到麦隆晶格的转变以及扭曲诱导的麦隆团簇。

Result: 成功构建了声学斯格明子袋，其拓扑数和几何构型可被扭转角与旋转中心确定性调制。在有限缺陷密度范围内，莫尔斯格明子袋保持复合拓扑；与未扭曲的单层斯格明子晶格相比，在高缺陷密度下内嵌斯格明子团簇展现出更高的稳定性。平台还实现了斯格明子晶格到麦隆晶格的可逆转变及扭曲诱导麦隆团簇。

Conclusion: 声学莫尔超晶格可作为可重构平台用于工程化稳健的实空间拓扑织构，在拓扑引导的声学操控和信息编码方面具有潜在应用前景。

Abstract: Moire superlattices formed by twisting periodic systems provide a powerful platform for emergent topological phenomena, but their use for programming real-space topology in acoustic wave fields remains largely unexplored. Here we report a phase-controlled spoof surface acoustic wave platform for constructing moire topological textures in the acoustic particle-velocity field. On a perforated acoustic metasurface, two twisted skyrmion lattices are synthesized and superposed, yielding acoustic skyrmion bags with controllable topological numbers and geometries. The twist angle and rotation center control the scale and configuration of the bags, enabling deterministic reshaping of the composite texture. We further introduce controlled defects to assess the defect tolerance of the moire skyrmion bags. The skyrmion bags retain their composite topology over a finite range of defect densities, and comparison with an untwisted single-layer skyrmion lattice suggests enhanced stability of the enclosed skyrmion cluster relative to the single-layer reference at higher defect densities. The same programmable platform also supports transitions from skyrmion lattices to meron lattices and enables twist-induced meron clusters. These findings establish acoustic moire superlattices as a reconfigurable platform for engineering robust real-space topological textures, with potential applications in topology-guided acoustic manipulation and information encoding.

</details>


### [141] [Reduced Dark Current in Cd0.9Zn0.1Te Detector Arrays by Aluminium Oxide Passivation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02516)
*Sudarshan Singh,Luc Montpetit,Eloïse Rahier,Mahmoud R. M. Atalla,Sebastian Koelling,Oussama Moutanabbir*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本文推翻了"碲锌镉（CZT）半导体在150°C以上加工会导致不可逆器件退化"的传统观点，证明在250°C下通过原子层沉积（ALD）制备的Al2O3钝化层不仅能保持CZT探测器性能，还能显著改善其性能：暗电流降低约5倍、像素间漏电流从~41 nA降至~2-3 nA、电流-电压迟滞完全消除，且MS与MIS探测器钝化后性能无显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为CZT半导体在150°C以上处理会导致不可逆器件退化，这限制了高温工艺在高性能CZT探测器中的应用；作者旨在挑战这一认知，探索高温ALD钝化工艺是否可行并能否提升探测器性能。

Method: 在250°C下通过原子层沉积（ALD）在像素化金属-半导体（MS）和金属-绝缘体-半导体（MIS）CZT探测器上沉积薄层Al2O3钝化膜，随后进行电学表征，包括高偏压（1000V）运行测试、暗电流测量、像素间漏电流测量、电流-电压（I-V）迟滞分析以及MS与MIS结构对比。

Result: 钝化器件在1000V高偏压下无退化；暗电流降低约5倍；像素间漏电流从~41 nA降至~2-3 nA（-200V），降低近一个数量级；相邻像素暗电流特性高度均匀；I-V迟滞完全消除；MS与MIS探测器钝化后暗电流无显著差异。

Conclusion: 250°C下的优化Al2O3钝化工艺与高性能CZT探测器加工完全兼容，可抑制缺陷辅助的电荷输运，为更低噪声、更高谱分辨率的X射线成像系统提供了切实可行的路径。

Abstract: Contrary to the prevailing view that processing cadmium zinc telluride (CZT) semiconductors above 150 °C leads to irreversible device degradation, here we show that Al2O3 passivation layer deposited by atomic layer deposition at 250 °C not only preserves CZT detector performance but also substantially improves it. Pixelated metal-semiconductor (MS) and metal-insulator-semiconductor (MIS) CZT detectors were passivated with a thin atomic-layer-deposited Al2O3 film and characterized electrically. The passivated devices exhibited no measurable degradation, even under a high-bias operation of 1000 V. Instead, the dark current decreased by approximately a factor of five, while the interpixel leakage current was reduced by nearly one order of magnitude, from ~41 nA to ~2-3 nA at -200 V. Passivation also produced highly uniform dark-current characteristics across adjacent pixels and completely eliminated current-voltage hysteresis, indicating suppression of defect-assisted charge transport. Furthermore, no significant difference in dark current was observed between the MS and MIS detectors after passivation, suggesting that the Al2O3 layer dominates the surface electrical behavior. These results demonstrate that optimized Al2O3 passivation at 250 °C is fully compatible with high-performance CZT detector processing and provides a practical route toward lower-noise, higher-spectral-resolution X-ray imaging systems.

</details>
